📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-28  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.01372-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0137226

牛粘病毒抗性蛋白 (Mx)1(但不是 Mx2)介导 抗病毒药物 针对人类和 禽流感 A 病毒(包括 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的活性

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b
作者： Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yong-Yan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria A. Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒最近感染了美国的奶牛,引起了对人类 跨物种溢出 的担忧。牛 宿主 防御因子(包括粘病毒抗性蛋白(bMx1 和 bMx2))的 抗病毒药物 作用仍相对未得到充分研究。我们检测了 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估了它们的 抗病毒药物 活性。bMx1 和 bMx2 在乳腺细胞中的诱导作用比在气管细胞中的诱导作用更强。bMx1 而非 bMx2 的过表达可显着抑制季节性甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、低致病性和 高致病性禽流感 病毒,包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b,但不能抑制乙型流感病毒。相反,bMx1 的敲低导致 IAV 生长增强。此外,bMx1 抑制病毒复制的早期阶段,从而减少病毒转录和病毒复制周期的后续步骤。已知的 突变 可以降低人类 MxA (hMxA) 活性,同时也会损害 bMx1 功能,而与 hMxA 抗性相关的病毒核蛋白 (NP) 中的 突变 也会降低病毒对 bMx1 的敏感性。此外,系统发育分析表明,一小部分(>0.03%)禽类 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 病毒已经携带其中一些 NP 突变。这些发现表明,虽然当前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或机会性重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。重要性 高致病性禽流感 病毒已扩散到奶牛中,并自 2024 年以来一直在美国奶牛场传播。奶牛对流感病毒的先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 尚未得到广泛研究。在这项研究中,我们重点关注 Mx 蛋白,它是针对病毒的先天免疫防御系统的关键部分。我们专门研究了牛 Mx 蛋白,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对不同流感病毒(包括高致病性毒株)的活性。我们还表明,牛 Mx1 的 抗病毒药物 活性机制与人类等效的 MxA 蛋白非常相似。此外,我们发现这些禽类病毒中的一小部分具有与牛 Mx1 和人 MxA 蛋白抗性相关的 突变。总体而言,这项工作强调了高致病性流感病毒通过在牛(一种与人类关系密切的哺乳动物物种)中持续传播而增加的大流行风险。

设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们重点关注 Mx 蛋白,它是针对病毒的先天免疫防御系统的关键部分。
核心方法
我们检测了 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估了它们的 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
此外,系统发育分析表明,一小部分(>0.03%)禽类 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 病毒已经携带其中一些 NP 突变。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,虽然当前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或机会性重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。
科研与公卫意义
牛对流感病毒的先天性 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 尚未得到广泛研究。

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b

Authors: Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yong-Yan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria A. Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have recently infected dairy cows in the United States, raising concerns about human spillover. The antiviral roles of bovine host defense factors, including myxovirus resistance proteins (bMx1 and bMx2), remain relatively understudied. We examined bMx1/bMx2 induction in differentiated bovine cells following HPAI H5N1 infection and assessed their antiviral activity using overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis approaches. Both bMx1 and bMx2 were more strongly induced in mammary cells than in tracheal cells. Overexpression of bMx1, but not bMx2, significantly inhibited seasonal influenza A viruses (IAVs), low-pathogenic, and highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b, but not influenza B viruses. Conversely, knockdown of bMx1 resulted in enhanced IAV growth. Further, bMx1 inhibited early stages of viral replication, acting to reduce viral transcription and subsequent steps in the viral replication cycle. Known mutations that reduce human MxA (hMxA) activity also impaired bMx1 function, and mutations in the viral nucleoprotein (NP) associated with resistance to hMxA also reduced virus sensitivity to bMx1. Furthermore, phylogenetic analysis showed that a small minority (>0.03%) of avian H5N1 2.3.4.4b viruses already carry some of these NP mutations. These findings suggest that while current H5N1 viruses largely remain susceptible to bMx1, viral evolution or opportunistic reassortment could drive Mx resistance and increase pandemic risk. IMPORTANCE Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses have spilled over into dairy cows and have been circulating on dairy farms in the United States since 2024. The innate antiviral immune responses of cows to influenza viruses have not been extensively studied. In this study, we focused on Mx proteins, a key part of the innate immune defense system toward viruses. We specifically looked at bovine Mx proteins and demonstrated antiviral activity toward different influenza viruses, including the highly pathogenic strains. We also showed that the mechanism of antiviral activity of bovine Mx1 was very similar to that of the human equivalent MxA protein. Further, we found that a small minority of these avian viruses have mutations associated with resistance to both bovine Mx1 and human MxA proteins. Overall, this work highlights the elevated pandemic risk for highly pathogenic influenza viruses through continual circulation in cows, a mammalian species in close proximity to humans.

编码多聚化 AIV-M2e 的 mRNA 疫苗 与 MHC-II 不变链基序和 Pan-DR 表位融合,赋予鸡针对 H9N2 病毒的保护作用

mRNA vaccine encoding multimerized AIV-M2e fused to MHC-II invariant chain motif and Pan-DR epitope confers protection against H9N2 virus in chickens
作者： Sandeep Yadav, Aparna Varma, Shraman Jana, Samiran Mondal, Shayan Sharif, Amirul Islam Mallick
摘要中文翻译禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 仍然是全球家禽健康和生产的主要威胁。不同AIV亚型之间的跨物种传播风险对疾病控制构成了重大挑战,这凸显了对提供广泛而持久保护的疫苗的需求。然而,现有的灭活和基于 媒介 的 疫苗 提供有限的跨亚型覆盖和针对抗原多样化 AIV 的持久免疫力。相比之下,与传统平台相比,针对 SARS-CoV-2 的 mRNA 疫苗 的成功通过实现快速、灵活和可扩展的 疫苗 设计,加速了 疫苗 的开发。我们设计了一个 mRNA 疫苗,编码保守的流感 M2 胞外域 (4xM2e) 的四个串联重复,与鸡不变链 (Ii) 基序和 13 个氨基酸通用 Pan-DR 结合表位 (PADRE) 融合。我们假设这种设计将增强抗 M2e 抗体反应,同时促进有效的抗原加工和 CD4 + T 辅助细胞激活,从而诱导鸡针对 AIV 感染的全身和粘膜免疫。mRNA 构建体通过标准脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 递送,并在肌内 (IM) 和鼻内 (IN) 给药后评估 疫苗 功效。我们的数据表明,全身和粘膜抗体反应适度增加,同时 T 细胞增殖增强,CD4 + 和 γδ + T 细胞群增加。在用 A/turkey/Wisconsin/1/1966/H9N2 病毒攻击后,无论采用何种给药途径,接种疫苗的禽类均表现出病毒脱落显着减少。组织病理学分析进一步证明肺结构基本保留且炎症最小,证明了粘膜屏障完整性的有效维持。为了评估抗 M2e 抗体的交叉保护潜力和功能活性,我们证明了 PR8/H1N1 的体外异亚型识别,并通过抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 证实了它们的功能相关性。总之,我们的研究结果表明,目前的 mRNA 疫苗 构建体编码与 PADRE 和 Ii 基序融合的结构优化的多聚化 M2e 蛋白,代表了一种安全有效的 疫苗 策略,有可能赋予鸡针对 AIV 感染的广泛保护性免疫力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 仍然是全球家禽健康和生产的主要威胁。不同AIV亚型之间的跨物种传播风险对疾病控制构成了重大挑战,这凸显了对提供广泛而持久保护的疫苗的需求。
设计与对象
我们设计了一个 mRNA 疫苗,编码保守的流感 M2 胞外域 (4xM2e) 的四个串联重复,与鸡不变链 (Ii) 基序和 13 个氨基酸通用 Pan-DR 结合表位 (PADRE) 融合。mRNA 构建体通过标准脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 递送,并在肌内 (IM) 和鼻内 (IN) 给药后评估 疫苗 功效。
核心方法
我们的数据表明,全身和粘膜抗体反应适度增加,同时 T 细胞增殖增强,CD4 + 和 γδ + T 细胞群增加。
主要结果
在用 A/turkey/Wisconsin/1/1966/H9N2 病毒攻击后,无论采用何种给药途径,接种疫苗的禽类均表现出病毒脱落显着减少。组织病理学分析进一步证明肺结构基本保留且炎症最小,证明了粘膜屏障完整性的有效维持。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果表明,目前的 mRNA 疫苗 构建体编码与 PADRE 和 Ii 基序融合的结构优化的多聚化 M2e 蛋白,代表了一种安全有效的 疫苗 策略,有可能赋予鸡针对 AIV 感染的广泛保护性免疫力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地为 禽流感 的基于 mRNA 的 疫苗 的开发提供了信息,该 疫苗 可以针对抗原多样化的 AIV 提供广泛而持久的保护,可能改善家禽疾病控制并降低人畜共患 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体限制。

mRNA vaccine encoding multimerized AIV-M2e fused to MHC-II invariant chain motif and Pan-DR epitope confers protection against H9N2 virus in chickens

Authors: Sandeep Yadav, Aparna Varma, Shraman Jana, Samiran Mondal, Shayan Sharif, Amirul Islam Mallick
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza viruses (AIVs) remain a major threat to global poultry health and production. The risk of cross-species transmission among diverse AIV subtypes poses a significant challenge to disease control, underscoring the need for vaccines that provide broad and durable protection. However, existing inactivated and vector-based vaccines provide limited cross-subtype coverage and durable immunity against antigenically diverse AIVs. In contrast, the success of mRNA vaccines against SARS-CoV-2 has accelerated vaccine development by enabling rapid, flexible, and scalable vaccine design compared with conventional platforms. We designed an mRNA vaccine encoding four tandem repeats of the conserved influenza M2 ectodomain (4xM2e), fused to the chicken invariant chain (Ii) motif and a 13-amino-acid universal Pan-DR-binding epitope (PADRE). We hypothesized that this design would enhance anti-M2e antibody responses while promoting efficient antigen processing and CD4 + T-helper cell activation, thereby inducing systemic and mucosal immunity against AIV infection in chickens. The mRNA construct was delivered via standard lipid nanoparticles (LNPs), and vaccine efficacy was evaluated following intramuscular (IM) and intranasal (IN) administration. Our data demonstrated a modest increase in systemic and mucosal antibody responses, accompanied by enhanced T-cell proliferation and increased CD4 + and γδ + T-cell populations. Following challenge with A/turkey/Wisconsin/1/1966/H9N2 virus, vaccinated birds exhibited significantly reduced viral shedding regardless of the route of administration. Histopathological analysis further demonstrated largely preserved pulmonary architecture with minimal inflammation, demonstrating effective maintenance of mucosal barrier integrity. To assess the cross-protective potential and functional activity of anti-M2e antibodies, we demonstrated in vitro heterosubtypic recognition of PR8/H1N1 and confirmed their functional relevance by antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC). Together, our findings demonstrate that the present mRNA vaccine construct, encoding a structurally optimized multimerized M2e protein fused to PADRE and the Ii motif, represents a safe and effective vaccine strategy with the potential to confer broad protective immunity against AIV infection in chickens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza viruses (AIVs) remain a major threat to global poultry health and production. The risk of cross-species transmission among diverse AIV subtypes poses a significant challenge to disease control, underscoring the need for vaccines that provide broad and durable protection.
Design and population
We designed an mRNA vaccine encoding four tandem repeats of the conserved influenza M2 ectodomain (4xM2e), fused to the chicken invariant chain (Ii) motif and a 13-amino-acid universal Pan-DR-binding epitope (PADRE). The mRNA construct was delivered via standard lipid nanoparticles (LNPs), and vaccine efficacy was evaluated following intramuscular (IM) and intranasal (IN) administration.
Core methods
Our data demonstrated a modest increase in systemic and mucosal antibody responses, accompanied by enhanced T-cell proliferation and increased CD4 + and γδ + T-cell populations.
Main results
Following challenge with A/turkey/Wisconsin/1/1966/H9N2 virus, vaccinated birds exhibited significantly reduced viral shedding regardless of the route of administration. Histopathological analysis further demonstrated largely preserved pulmonary architecture with minimal inflammation, demonstrating effective maintenance of mucosal barrier integrity.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings demonstrate that the present mRNA vaccine construct, encoding a structurally optimized multimerized M2e protein fused to PADRE and the Ii motif, represents a safe and effective vaccine strategy with the potential to confer broad protective immunity against AIV infection in chickens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform the development of mRNA-based vaccines for avian influenza that could offer broad and durable protection against antigenically diverse AIVs, potentially improving disease control in poultry and reducing zoonotic transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or evidence strength assessment.

根据遗传相似性分配的甲型流感病毒 H5Nx 基因型与全球系统发育不一致

Influenza A virus H5Nx genotypes assigned by genetic similarity are incongruent with global phylogenies
作者： Angela McLaughlin, Hoc Tran, Deanna Tait, Hoang-Hai Nguyen, Josip Rudar, Jolene A. Giacinti, Anthony V. Signore, Oliver A Lung, Yohannes Berhane, Zvonimir Poljak
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 H5N1 和其他 H5 亚型 (H5Nx) 具有广泛的 宿主 范围,并构成大流行风险。自2022年初以来,H5进化枝2.3.4.4b在全球范围内占据主导地位,在野生鸟类、家禽和哺乳动物中造成前所未有的死亡率,对人类也造成了跨物种溢出。准确的病毒分类对于追踪 H5Nx 变异的出现和传播至关重要,但具有不同进化历史的 基因组 片段之间的重排使基因分型变得复杂。H5Nx 基因型分配工具(北美的 GenoFlu 和欧洲的 Genin2)根据与参考序列的遗传相似性将片段分配给簇,并将基因型定义为片段簇的星座。然而,遗传相似性并不一定意味着最近的共同祖先,因为 突变 可能在树上多次出现,并且分支可能表现出不同的进化速率。此外,相似性是相对于可能不足以代表多样性的参考文献来定义的。我们评估了基于遗传相似性的 H5Nx 簇和基因型与八个片段的全局序列的系统发育的一致性。使用 GenoFlu 和 Genin2 对来自 41,366 个样本的公开 H5Nx 核苷酸序列进行基因型分配,并推断每个片段的最大似然系统发育。使用 TreeCluster 识别一系列父系距离阈值的系统发育簇,并使用一系列遗传相似性阈值识别 CD-HIT 簇。聚类方法根据系统发育纯度、单系性和多样性进行评估。主要 GenoFlu 和 Genin2 基因型内的重配根据其系统发育簇跨度进行评估。预计,从 2022 年到 2025 年,GenoFlu 基本上无法从北美、南美和南极洲以外的地区分配 基因组,而 Genin2 在欧洲、非洲和西亚以外的地区分配则很困难。只有 14.2% 的 GenoFlu 簇是单系的,而 Genin2 簇在各个区段之间变化更大(总体而言,49.3% 是单系的)。尽管 CD-HIT 产生的遗传距离簇与 TreeCluster 大小相似,但它们的单系性较低 (44%),并且包含更大的系统发育多样性。我们鉴定了几种基因型,例如 GenoFlu B3.13、D1.1 和 D1.3,其内部片段已多样化为连续的簇。同样,GenoFlu 基因型 B3.5 和 A3 以及 Genin2 基因型 EA-2020-C、EA-2021-AB 和 EA-2022-CH 的广泛系统发育跨度表明它们可能错误地组合了多个重配体。基于遗传相似性的 H5Nx 基因分型工具快速、稳定且足以进行广泛的分类,但可能会对具有不同进化历史的病毒进行分组,而无法分配不同的病毒,从而存在 监测、建模和风险评估存在偏见的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
根据遗传相似性分配的甲型流感病毒 H5Nx 基因型可能与全球系统发育不一致,因为这些病毒具有广泛的 宿主 范围并构成大流行风险。
设计与对象
该研究使用来自 41,366 个样本的公开 H5Nx 核苷酸序列,评估了基于遗传相似性的 H5Nx 簇和基因型与八个片段的全局序列系统发育的一致性。
核心方法
根据与参考序列的遗传相似性,使用 GenoFlu 和 Genin2 分配基因型,并使用 TreeCluster 和 CD-HIT 以及不同的父系距离和遗传相似性阈值来识别系统发育簇。
主要结果
GenoFlu 簇表现出较低的单系性(14.2%),而 Genin2 簇的变异性更大(整体单系性为 49.3%)。CD-HIT 簇的单系性较低 (44%),并且包含更大的系统发育多样性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,基于遗传相似性的基因分型可能会对具有不同进化历史的病毒进行分组,而无法分配不同的病毒,从而可能导致 监测 和风险评估出现偏差。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提醒人们不要过度依赖基于遗传相似性的基因分型来追踪 H5Nx 变异,因为它可能会掩盖真正的进化关系并妨碍准确的风险评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对公开序列的依赖以及基于遗传相似性的分类的固有挑战的限制,由于可变的 突变 率和进化历史,该分类可能无法捕获最近的共同祖先。

Influenza A virus H5Nx genotypes assigned by genetic similarity are incongruent with global phylogenies

Authors: Angela McLaughlin, Hoc Tran, Deanna Tait, Hoang-Hai Nguyen, Josip Rudar, Jolene A. Giacinti, Anthony V. Signore, Oliver A Lung, Yohannes Berhane, Zvonimir Poljak
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus H5N1 and other H5 subtypes (H5Nx) have broad host ranges and pose a pandemic risk. Since early 2022, H5 clade 2.3.4.4b has predominated globally, causing unprecedented mortality in wild birds, poultry, and mammals, with spillovers to humans. Accurate viral classification is essential for tracking H5Nx variant emergence and spread, but reassortment among genome segments with distinct evolutionary histories complicates genotyping. H5Nx genotype assignment tools, GenoFlu in North America and Genin2 in Europe, assign segments to clusters by genetic similarity to reference sequences and define genotypes as constellations of segment clusters. However, genetic similarity does not necessarily imply recent shared ancestry because mutations may occur multiple times across the tree and branches may exhibit variable evolutionary rates. Furthermore, similarity is defined relative to references that may inadequately represent diversity. We evaluated the congruence of genetic similarity-based H5Nx clusters and genotypes with phylogenies of global sequences across eight segments. Publicly available H5Nx nucleotide sequences from 41,366 samples were assigned genotypes using GenoFlu and Genin2, and maximum likelihood phylogenies were inferred for each segment. Phylogenetic clusters were identified using TreeCluster for a range of patristic distance thresholds and CD-HIT clusters were identified for a range of genetic similarity thresholds. Clustering methods were evaluated based on phylogenetic purity, monophyly, and diversity. Reassortment within predominant GenoFlu and Genin2 genotypes was evaluated based on their phylogenetic cluster spans. Expectedly, GenoFlu was largely unable to assign genomes from outside of North America, South America, and Antarctica from 2022 to 2025, whereas Genin2 assignments struggled outside of Europe, Africa, and Western Asia. Only 14.2% of GenoFlu clusters were monophyletic, while Genin2 clusters were more variable across segments (overall 49.3% monophyletic). Despite CD-HIT yielding genetic distance clusters of similar size to TreeCluster, they were less monophyletic (44%) and encompassed greater phylogenetic diversity. We identified several genotypes, such as GenoFlu B3.13, D1.1, and D1.3, with internal segments that had diversified into contiguous clusters. Similarly, the wide phylogenetic span of GenoFlu genotypes B3.5 and A3, and Genin2 genotypes EA-2020-C, EA-2021-AB, and EA-2022-CH suggest they may erroneously combine multiple reassortants. Genetic similarity-based H5Nx genotyping tools are rapid, stable, and sufficient for broad categorization, but may group viruses with distinct evolutionary histories and fail to assign divergent viruses, risking biased surveillance, modeling, and risk assessment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus H5Nx genotypes assigned by genetic similarity may not align with global phylogenies, as these viruses have broad host ranges and pose a pandemic risk.
Design and population
The study evaluated the congruence of genetic similarity-based H5Nx clusters and genotypes with phylogenies of global sequences across eight segments using publicly available H5Nx nucleotide sequences from 41,366 samples.
Core methods
Genotypes were assigned using GenoFlu and Genin2 based on genetic similarity to reference sequences, and phylogenetic clusters were identified using TreeCluster and CD-HIT with varying patristic distance and genetic similarity thresholds.
Main results
GenoFlu clusters showed low monophyly (14.2%), while Genin2 clusters were more variable (49.3% overall monophyletic). CD-HIT clusters were less monophyletic (44%) and included greater phylogenetic diversity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that genetic similarity-based genotyping may group viruses with distinct evolutionary histories and fail to assign divergent viruses, potentially leading to biased surveillance and risk assessment.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results caution against over-reliance on genetic similarity-based genotyping for tracking H5Nx variants, as it may obscure true evolutionary relationships and hinder accurate risk assessment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on publicly available sequences and the inherent challenges of genetic similarity-based classification, which may not capture recent shared ancestry due to variable mutation rates and evolutionary histories.

雪貂体内毛皮海豹衍生的基因型 A3 甲型流感 (H5N1) 病毒的接触 传播

Contact transmission of fur seal-derived genotype A3 influenza A(H5N1) virus in ferrets
作者： Andrey S. Gudymo, Natalia P. Kolosova, Anastasia S. Panova, Kiunnei N. Shadrinova, Natalia I. Goncharova, Olga N. Perfilieva, Natalia V. Danilchenko, Anastasia A. Moiseeva, Elena I. Danilenko, Natalia N. Vasiltsova
摘要中文翻译进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒的全球传播伴随着针对哺乳动物的跨物种 传播 事件频率的增加。2023 年,俄罗斯远东地区记录到北方海狗(Callorhinus ursinus)大规模流行,这与携带适应性取代 PB2-K482R 和 NP-N319K 的 A3 基因型有关。该病毒在小鼠和雪貂中均表现出高毒力。为了评估这种重配体的传播潜力,我们在雪貂动物模型中通过实验研究了其接触 传播 的能力。在与幼崽共同饲养期间观察到 传播 的部分接触:四名接受者中的三名出现感染的临床症状,一名接受者检测到活跃的病毒复制,两名幸存者证实血清转化。在受感染的供体雪貂的病毒中检测到了潜在的从头哺乳动物适应性 突变 NS-I81T。这项研究提供了第一个实验证据,证明哺乳动物中基因型 A3 的进化枝 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) 分离株具有接触 传播 的能力,与特定的适应分子标记的存在相关。这些发现强调了优先考虑循环变异的功能和 基因组监测 的必要性,以便及时评估其人畜共患风险和持续跨物种循环的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查海狗衍生的基因型 A3 甲型流感 (H5N1) 病毒在哺乳动物中的传播潜力,解决人们对 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒跨物种 传播 事件日益增长的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究于 2023 年进行,使用雪貂动物模型,通过实验评估与特定适应分子标记相关的基因型 A3 A(H5N1) 病毒的接触 传播 能力。
核心方法
该病毒在雪貂中进行了接触 传播 的实验测试,并使用共同饲养来评估其在未接触过的动物之间传播的能力。
主要结果
观察到部分接触 传播,四名接受者中的三名出现感染的临床症状,一名接受者检测到活跃的病毒复制,两名幸存者确认血清转化。
解释与创新
研究结果证明了这种基因型 A3 A(H5N1) 病毒在哺乳动物之间传播的潜力,强调了与哺乳动物适应相关的分子标记的存在。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了监测基因型 A3 A(H5N1) 病毒的人畜共患潜力和适应哺乳动物 宿主 的能力的重要性,这可以为 宿主 的公共卫生策略和风险评估提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究提供了雪貂接触传播的实验证据,但其发现仅限于该动物模型,由于缺乏更广泛的宿主或现场数据,证据强度中等。

Contact transmission of fur seal-derived genotype A3 influenza A(H5N1) virus in ferrets

Authors: Andrey S. Gudymo, Natalia P. Kolosova, Anastasia S. Panova, Kiunnei N. Shadrinova, Natalia I. Goncharova, Olga N. Perfilieva, Natalia V. Danilchenko, Anastasia A. Moiseeva, Elena I. Danilenko, Natalia N. Vasiltsova
Original abstract (English)
The global spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) virus of clade 2.3.4.4b is accompanied by an increasing frequency of cross-species transmission events to mammals. In 2023, a mass epizootic among northern fur seals (Callorhinus ursinus) was recorded in the Russian Far East, associated with genotype A3 carrying the adaptive substitutions PB2-K482R and NP-N319K. The virus demonstrated high virulence in both mice and ferrets. To evaluate the transmissive potential of this reassortant, we experimentally investigated its capacity for contact transmission in a ferret animal model. Partial contact transmission was observed during co-housing with naïve animals: clinical signs of infection developed in three of four recipients, active viral replication was detected in one, and seroconversion was confirmed in two survivors. Potential de novo mammalian-adaptative mutation NS-I81T was detected in the virus from an infected donor ferret. This study provides the first experimental evidence of contact transmission capability among mammals for a clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) isolate of genotype A3, correlating with the presence of specific molecular markers of adaptation. These findings underscore the necessity of prioritizing functional and genomic surveillance of circulating variants to enable timely assessment of their zoonotic risk and potential for sustained cross-species circulation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the transmissive potential of a fur seal-derived genotype A3 influenza A(H5N1) virus in mammals, addressing the growing concern about cross-species transmission events of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) viruses.
Design and population
The study was conducted in 2023 using a ferret animal model to experimentally evaluate the contact transmission capability of a genotype A3 A(H5N1) virus associated with specific molecular markers of adaptation.
Core methods
The virus was experimentally tested for contact transmission in ferrets, with co-housing used to assess its capacity to spread between naïve animals.
Main results
Partial contact transmission was observed, with clinical signs of infection developing in three of four recipients, active viral replication detected in one, and seroconversion confirmed in two survivors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the potential for this genotype A3 A(H5N1) virus to transmit between mammals, highlighting the presence of molecular markers associated with mammalian adaptation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of monitoring genotype A3 A(H5N1) viruses for their zoonotic potential and ability to adapt to mammalian hosts, which could inform public health strategies for surveillance and risk assessment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides experimental evidence of contact transmission in ferrets, but its findings are limited to this animal model, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of broader host or field data.

针对 H5N1 流感病毒的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 疫苗 可保护泌乳奶牛免受感染

An mRNA–lipid nanoparticle vaccine against H5N1 influenza virus protects lactating dairy cows from infection
作者： Jefferson José da Silva Santos, Carine K. Souza, Giovana Ciacci Zanella, Débora Brito Goulart, Bailey L. Arruda, Paola Mercedes Boggiatto, Mitchell V. Palmer, Celeste A. Snyder, Michaela A. Kristula, Charlene M. Dickens
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒最近在美国奶牛中出现,导致乳腺炎和牛奶产量急剧下降。H5N1 病毒复制的主要部位是乳腺组织而不是呼吸道,动物之间的病毒 传播 被认为是通过牛奶中的病毒脱落而发生的。在这里,我们生成了编码 A/奶牛/德克萨斯/24-008749-002/2024(代表循环牛 H5N1 病毒)的血凝素 (HA) 的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,并评估其在泌乳奶牛中的免疫原性和功效。将八头奶牛分为疫苗组和对照组,接受两次肌肉注射剂量的 疫苗 或生理盐水,然后进行乳房内病毒攻击。疫苗接种 引发血清中的 HA 反应性抗体和中和抗体以及牛奶中的 HA 反应性抗体。病毒攻击后,接种疫苗的奶牛牛奶中病毒 RNA 峰值降低约 1,000 倍,乳腺炎和乳腺纤维化的发生率也较低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在这里,我们生成了编码 A/奶牛/德克萨斯/24-008749-002/2024(代表循环牛 H5N1 病毒)的血凝素 (HA) 的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,并评估其在泌乳奶牛中的免疫原性和功效。
设计与对象
H5N1 病毒复制的主要部位是乳腺组织而不是呼吸道,动物之间的病毒 传播 被认为是通过牛奶中的病毒脱落而发生的。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
病毒攻击后,接种疫苗的奶牛牛奶中病毒 RNA 峰值降低约 1,000 倍,乳腺炎和乳腺纤维化的发生率也较低。
科研与公卫意义
疫苗接种 引发血清中的 HA 反应性抗体和中和抗体以及牛奶中的 HA 反应性抗体。
局限与证据强度
将八头奶牛分为疫苗组和对照组,接受两次肌肉注射剂量的 疫苗 或生理盐水,然后进行乳房内病毒攻击。

An mRNA–lipid nanoparticle vaccine against H5N1 influenza virus protects lactating dairy cows from infection

Authors: Jefferson José da Silva Santos, Carine K. Souza, Giovana Ciacci Zanella, Débora Brito Goulart, Bailey L. Arruda, Paola Mercedes Boggiatto, Mitchell V. Palmer, Celeste A. Snyder, Michaela A. Kristula, Charlene M. Dickens
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 virus has recently emerged in dairy cattle in the USA, leading to mastitis and sharp reductions in milk production. The main site of H5N1 viral replication is the mammary tissue rather than the respiratory tract and viral transmission between animals is thought to occur through viral shedding in milk. Here, we generate an mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccine encoding the hemagglutinin (HA) of A/dairy cattle/Texas/24-008749-002/2024, representative of circulating bovine H5N1 viruses, and assess its immunogenicity and efficacy in lactating dairy cows. Eight cows were divided into vaccinated and control groups, receiving two intramuscular doses of the vaccine or saline, followed by intramammary viral challenge. Vaccination elicited HA-reactive and neutralizing antibodies in serum and HA-reactive antibodies in milk. Following viral challenge, vaccinated cows had ~1,000-fold lower peak viral RNA in milk and a lower occurrence of mastitis and mammary fibrosis.

Here, we generate an mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccine encoding the hemagglutinin (HA) of A/dairy cattle/Texas/24-008749-002/2024, representative of circulating bovine H5N1 viruses, and assess its immunogenicity and efficacy in lactating dairy cows.
Design and population
The main site of H5N1 viral replication is the mammary tissue rather than the respiratory tract and viral transmission between animals is thought to occur through viral shedding in milk.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
Following viral challenge, vaccinated cows had ~1,000-fold lower peak viral RNA in milk and a lower occurrence of mastitis and mammary fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

抗体 3CF5 通过靶向保守的非受体结合血凝素表位来中和历史和新出现的 H5Nx 流感病毒

Antibody 3CF5 neutralizes historical and emerging H5Nx influenza viruses by targeting a conserved non-receptor-binding hemagglutinin epitope
作者： Huan Liang, Chuanying Niu, Yuanyi Cheng, Kong Yen Liew, Linbing Qu, Zhiqiang Zheng, Wenyi Yang, Qian Wang, Lang Wei, Banghui Liu
摘要中文翻译H5Nx 亚型的 禽流感 病毒构成持续的大流行威胁。在这里,我们用 H5N6 疫苗 免疫恒河猴,获得了 30 种 H5 反应性单克隆抗体 (mAb)。三种 IgG mAb(3CF5、3BG3 和 3BE7)表现出针对多种 H5Nx 病毒的有效中和活性,并在致死小鼠攻击模型中提供保护。值得注意的是,3CF5 在 1997 年至 2024 年期间表现出对 H5Nx 病毒的广泛中和作用,包括与奶牛中正在进行的 暴发 相关的当代分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 菌株。冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 分析显示,3CF5 靶向血凝素 (HA) 头上的保守非受体结合表位,跨越残留酯酶结构域和相邻的侧面。3CF5 主要通过抑制膜融合来介导中和,并且还具有抗体依赖性细胞毒性。此外,3CF5-IgG同种型转化为粘膜分泌型IgA1 (sIgA1),对H5N1病毒的中和效力增强了128倍,凸显了sIgA1作为鼻腔给药预防剂的优势。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H5Nx 亚型的 禽流感 病毒构成持续的大流行威胁。这项研究旨在鉴定能够有效中和这些病毒的抗体。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 H5N6 疫苗 对恒河猴进行免疫,并获得 30 种 H5 反应性单克隆抗体 (mAb)。然后对这些 mAb 进行针对多种 H5Nx 病毒的测试,包括与奶牛中正在发生的 暴发 相关的当代分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 菌株。
核心方法
测试了三种 IgG mAb(3CF5、3BG3 和 3BE7)针对 H5Nx 病毒的中和活性。使用冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 分析来确定 3CF5 靶向的表位,并评估同种型转化为粘膜分泌型 IgA1 (sIgA1) 的增强中和效力。
主要结果
三种 IgG mAb(3CF5、3BG3 和 3BE7)表现出针对多种 H5Nx 病毒的有效中和活性,并在致死小鼠攻击模型中提供保护。值得注意的是,3CF5 在 1997 年至 2024 年期间表现出对 H5Nx 病毒的广泛中和作用。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 3CF5 作为针对 H5Nx 病毒(包括新出现病毒株)的广谱中和抗体的潜力。该研究引入了一种新的中和机制,通过靶向血凝素 (HA) 头上的保守非受体结合表位。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,3CF5 可能是开发 疫苗 和针对 H5Nx 流感病毒的治疗剂的宝贵工具,特别是那些与牲畜中持续存在的 暴发 相关的病毒。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表现实世界的条件。然而,这些发现得到了详细的冷冻电镜分析和同种型转换实验的支持,提供了中等程度的置信度。

Antibody 3CF5 neutralizes historical and emerging H5Nx influenza viruses by targeting a conserved non-receptor-binding hemagglutinin epitope

Authors: Huan Liang, Chuanying Niu, Yuanyi Cheng, Kong Yen Liew, Linbing Qu, Zhiqiang Zheng, Wenyi Yang, Qian Wang, Lang Wei, Banghui Liu
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza viruses of the H5Nx subtype pose persistent pandemic threats. Here, we immunized rhesus macaques with an H5N6 vaccine and obtained 30 H5-reactive monoclonal antibodies (mAbs). Three IgG mAbs, 3CF5, 3BG3, and 3BE7, demonstrated potent neutralizing activity against multiple H5Nx viruses and conferred protection in a lethal mouse challenge model. Notably, 3CF5 exhibited broad neutralization against H5Nx viruses spanning 1997-2024, including contemporary clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 strains associated with an ongoing outbreak in dairy cattle. Cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) analysis revealed that 3CF5 targets a conserved non-receptor-binding epitope on the hemagglutinin (HA) head, spanning the vestigial esterase domain and an adjacent lateral surface. 3CF5 mediates neutralization primarily through inhibiting membrane fusion and also possesses antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity. Furthermore, isotype conversion of 3CF5-IgG to mucosal secretory IgA1 (sIgA1) enhanced neutralization potency against H5N1 viruses up to 128-fold, highlighting the advantage of sIgA1 as a nasally administered prophylactic agent.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza viruses of the H5Nx subtype pose persistent pandemic threats. This study aimed to identify antibodies that could neutralize these viruses effectively.
Design and population
The study involved immunizing rhesus macaques with an H5N6 vaccine and obtaining 30 H5-reactive monoclonal antibodies (mAbs). These mAbs were then tested against multiple H5Nx viruses, including contemporary clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 strains associated with an ongoing outbreak in dairy cattle.
Core methods
Three IgG mAbs, 3CF5, 3BG3, and 3BE7, were tested for neutralizing activity against H5Nx viruses. Cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) analysis was used to determine the epitope targeted by 3CF5, and isotype conversion to mucosal secretory IgA1 (sIgA1) was evaluated for enhanced neutralization potency.
Main results
Three IgG mAbs, 3CF5, 3BG3, and 3BE7, demonstrated potent neutralizing activity against multiple H5Nx viruses and conferred protection in a lethal mouse challenge model. Notably, 3CF5 exhibited broad neutralization against H5Nx viruses spanning 1997-2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the potential of 3CF5 as a broad-spectrum neutralizing antibody against H5Nx viruses, including emerging strains. The study introduces a novel mechanism of neutralization through targeting a conserved non-receptor-binding epitope on the hemagglutinin (HA) head.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that 3CF5 could be a valuable tool for developing vaccines and therapeutic agents against H5Nx influenza viruses, particularly those associated with ongoing outbreaks in livestock.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent real-world conditions. However, the findings are supported by detailed cryo-EM analysis and isotype conversion experiments, providing a moderate level of confidence.

哺乳动物来源的高致病性禽流感D1.1系H5N1毒株在雪貂中的传播效率差异

Variable transmission efficiency of mammalian origin HPAI D1.1 H5N1 strains in ferrets
作者： Grace E. Quirk, Michelle N. Vu, Valerie M. Le Sage, Kaitlyn Bushfield-Thomason, Hanh Dung Nguyen, Seema S. Lakdawala
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 基因型 D1.1 谱系继续在美国 野生鸟类 群体中占主导地位,并已三次独立地蔓延到奶牛中。为了评估该亚系的 传播 风险,我们对雪貂中的三种不同的 D1.1 品系进行了直接接触 传播 实验。其中两种菌株是从人类身上分离出来的,一种是从致命的猫感染中分离出来的。我们发现只有一种人类分离株(A/NV/10/2025)能够在雪貂之间有效传播。与其他菌株相比,该分离株具有哺乳动物适应性 PB2 D701N 突变,表明该 突变 可能是有助于增强 D1.1 的 传播 效率的候选菌株,而不是致命猫分离株中存在的 PB2 E627 K 取代。基于这些数据,我们得出结论,D1.1 菌株的 传播 适应性适中,但应特别注意 PB2 701 位置出现的适应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估哺乳动物来源的 HPAI D1.1 H5N1 菌株的 传播 风险,因为这些菌株在美国 野生鸟类 群体中继续占主导地位,并已三次独立地蔓延到奶牛中。
设计与对象
该研究对雪貂中的三种不同的 D1.1 品系进行了直接接触 传播 实验,以评估其 传播 效率。
核心方法
研究人员使用雪貂进行了直接接触 传播 实验,以评估三种不同 D1.1 菌株的 传播 风险,其中包括两种人类分离菌株和一种来自致命猫感染的菌株。
主要结果
只有一种人类分离株 (A/NV/10/2025) 能够在雪貂之间有效传播,而其他菌株则没有表现出有效的 传播。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,哺乳动物适应性 PB2 D701N 突变 可能有助于增强 D1.1 菌株的 传播 效率,这与致命猫分离株中存在的 PB2 E627K 取代相反。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了监测 D1.1 菌株中 PB2 701 传播 出现的重要性,这可能对其 传播 潜在和公共卫生风险产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于雪貂模型,并未考虑其他潜在的 宿主,但基于直接实验 传播 数据的证据是强有力的。

Variable transmission efficiency of mammalian origin HPAI D1.1 H5N1 strains in ferrets

Authors: Grace E. Quirk, Michelle N. Vu, Valerie M. Le Sage, Kaitlyn Bushfield-Thomason, Hanh Dung Nguyen, Seema S. Lakdawala
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1 lineage continues to predominate in the United States wild bird population and has spilled over into dairy cattle three independent times. To assess the transmission risk of this sublineage, we performed direct-contact transmission experiments for three distinct D1.1 strains in ferrets. Two of these strains were isolated from humans and one from a lethal cat infection. We found that only one human isolate (A/NV/10/2025) was able to transmit efficiently between ferrets. Compared to the other strains, this isolate harboured the mammalian adaptive PB2 D701N mutation, suggesting this mutation may be a candidate contributing to enhanced transmission efficiency of D1.1 as opposed to the PB2 E627 K substitution present in the lethal cat isolate. Based on these data, we conclude that the transmission fitness of D1.1 strains is modest, but special attention should be paid to emergence of adaptation at the PB2 701 position.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the transmission risk of mammalian origin HPAI D1.1 H5N1 strains, as these strains have continued to predominate in the United States wild bird population and have spilled over into dairy cattle three independent times.
Design and population
The study performed direct-contact transmission experiments for three distinct D1.1 strains in ferrets to evaluate their transmission efficiency.
Core methods
The researchers conducted direct-contact transmission experiments using ferrets to assess the transmission risk of three distinct D1.1 strains, including two human isolates and one from a lethal cat infection.
Main results
Only one human isolate (A/NV/10/2025) was able to transmit efficiently between ferrets, while the other strains did not show efficient transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the mammalian adaptive PB2 D701N mutation may contribute to enhanced transmission efficiency of D1.1 strains, as opposed to the PB2 E627K substitution present in the lethal cat isolate.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the importance of monitoring for the emergence of PB2 701 mutations in D1.1 strains, which could have implications for their transmission potential and public health risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to ferret models and does not account for other potential hosts, but the evidence is strong based on direct experimental transmission data.

绵羊、山羊和羊驼中 α2,3- 和 α2,6- 连接唾液酸受体的比较分布和甲型 (H5N1) 流感病毒结合

Comparative Distribution of α2,3- and α2,6-Linked Sialic Acid Receptors and Influenza A(H5N1) Virus Binding in Sheep, Goats, and Alpacas
作者： Nayrmaliz Marrero, Eli Blay, Serban Oancea, Gulnara Brixius, Rahul K. Nelli, Paul Joseph Plummer, Jennifer M. Groeltz-Thrush, Breigha Boyle, Brianna Jones, Olufemi O. Fasina
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) H5N1 继续对动物和人类健康构成重大威胁。自 2022 年以来,观察到 宿主 范围扩大,如奶牛中的 H5N1 暴发 所示。其他小型牲畜物种,如绵羊、山羊和羊驼,也首次检测出 H5N1 阳性,引发人们对其对先前被忽视的牲畜物种的影响的担忧。在这项研究中,我们首先使用山毛榉 (SA α2,3) 和接骨木 (SA α2,6) 凝集素表征了绵羊、山羊和羊驼的唾液酸 (SA) 受体在呼吸和非呼吸组织中的分布。我们发现这两种 SA 受体存在于不同物种的所有组织中,但其分布和丰度存在显着差异。接下来,我们在实验室适应的主干上开发了重组 LPAIV H5N1 病毒,以评估组织特异性病毒结合谱。结果表明,来自进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的代表性 H5N1 病毒可有效结合所有物种的呼吸道和非呼吸道组织。在绵羊中观察到最强的病毒结合,其次是山羊,最后是羊驼。在进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 LPAIV 版本和 Gs/Gd 谱系前体原型病毒中观察到类似的结合模式。最后,我们发现 H1N1 和 H3N2 病毒也与绵羊和山羊的呼吸道组织结合。这些发现为了解小型家畜物种对 HPAIV H5N1 和其他流感亚型的潜在易感性提供了重要见解。我们的结果有助于更好地了解跨牲畜物种的流感组织趋向性及其对人畜共患 传播 的潜在影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) H5N1 继续对动物和人类健康构成重大威胁,自 2022 年以来观察到的 宿主 范围有所扩大,包括绵羊、山羊和羊驼等小型牲畜物种。
设计与对象
该研究利用凝集素表征了绵羊、山羊和羊驼呼吸道和非呼吸道组织中唾液酸 (SA) 受体的分布,并评估了重组 LPAIV H5N1 病毒的组织特异性病毒结合谱。
核心方法
使用山槐 (SA α2,3) 和黑接骨木 (SA α2,6) 凝集素对组织进行染色以鉴定 SA 受体,并使用重组 LPAIV H5N1 病毒来评估病毒结合谱。
主要结果
两种 SA 受体均存在于不同物种的所有组织中,但其分布和丰度存在显着差异。H5N1 病毒在绵羊中的结合力最强,其次是山羊和羊驼。
解释与创新
这些发现为了解小型牲畜物种对 HPAIV H5N1 和其他流感亚型的潜在易感性提供了重要见解,有助于更好地了解跨牲畜物种的流感组织嗜性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 针对以前被忽视的牲畜物种中 HPAIV H5N1 的策略提供信息,从而有可能降低人畜共患 传播 的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到实验室适应性病毒的使用和缺乏直接临床结果的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Comparative Distribution of α2,3- and α2,6-Linked Sialic Acid Receptors and Influenza A(H5N1) Virus Binding in Sheep, Goats, and Alpacas

Authors: Nayrmaliz Marrero, Eli Blay, Serban Oancea, Gulnara Brixius, Rahul K. Nelli, Paul Joseph Plummer, Jennifer M. Groeltz-Thrush, Breigha Boyle, Brianna Jones, Olufemi O. Fasina
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) H5N1 continues to pose a significant threat to animal and human health. Since 2022, an expanded host range has been observed, as illustrated by the H5N1 outbreak in dairy cows. Other minor livestock species, such as sheep, goats, and alpacas, have also tested positive for H5N1 for the first time, raising concerns about its impact on previously overlooked livestock species. In this study, we first characterized the distribution of sialic acid (SA) receptors across respiratory and non-respiratory tissues of sheep, goats, and alpacas using Maackia amurensis (SA α2,3) and Sambucus nigra (SA α2,6) lectins. We found that both SA receptors are present in all tissues across species, with notable differences in their distribution and abundance. Next, we developed recombinant LPAIV H5N1 viruses over a laboratory-adapted backbone to evaluate tissue-specific virus binding profiles. The results show that a representative H5N1 virus from clade 2.3.4.4b binds effectively to respiratory and non-respiratory tissues across all species. The strongest virus binding was observed in sheep, followed by goats and, lastly, alpacas. Similar binding patterns were observed with an LPAIV version of clade 2.3.4.4b and a Gs/Gd lineage precursor prototype virus. Finally, we show that H1N1 and H3N2 viruses also bind to respiratory tissues of sheep and goats. These findings provide important insights into the potential susceptibility of minor livestock species to HPAIV H5N1 and other influenza subtypes. Our results contribute to a better understanding of influenza tissue tropism across livestock species and its potential implications for zoonotic transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) H5N1 continues to pose a significant threat to animal and human health, with an expanded host range observed since 2022, including minor livestock species such as sheep, goats, and alpacas.
Design and population
The study characterized the distribution of sialic acid (SA) receptors in respiratory and non-respiratory tissues of sheep, goats, and alpacas using lectins, and evaluated tissue-specific virus binding profiles with recombinant LPAIV H5N1 viruses.
Core methods
Tissues were stained with Maackia amurensis (SA α2,3) and Sambucus nigra (SA α2,6) lectins to identify SA receptors, and recombinant LPAIV H5N1 viruses were used to assess virus binding profiles.
Main results
Both SA receptors were present in all tissues across species, with notable differences in their distribution and abundance. H5N1 virus binding was strongest in sheep, followed by goats and alpacas.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide important insights into the potential susceptibility of minor livestock species to HPAIV H5N1 and other influenza subtypes, contributing to a better understanding of influenza tissue tropism across livestock species.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for HPAIV H5N1 in previously overlooked livestock species, potentially reducing the risk of zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of laboratory-adapted viruses and the absence of direct clinical outcomes, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

孟加拉国吉大港不同生产系统中 禽流感 病毒在鸽子和鹌鹑中传播的流行病学特征

Epidemiological characterization of avian influenza virus circulation in pigeons and quail across different production systems in Chattogram, Bangladesh
作者： Mohammad Iqbal Habib, Md. Abu Sayeed, Monjurul Islam, Mohammad Ferdous Rahman Sarker, Sydus Shalekin Shakil, Mohammad Mahmudul Hassan, Jade Kenneth Forwood, Abdul Ahad, Ariful Islam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解鸽子和鹌鹑在孟加拉国 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 传播中的作用,弥补这些被研究的家禽物种中 AIV 流行病学 的知识空白。
设计与对象
2021 年 11 月至 2022 年 4 月在孟加拉国吉大港进行了一项横断面研究,涉及来自家庭、商业农场和活禽市场的 300 只鸟类(108 只鸽子和 192 只鹌鹑)。
核心方法
从每只鸡身上收集口咽和泄殖腔拭子,并使用实时逆转录聚合酶链反应检测 AIV,然后对 H5、H7 和 H9 进行亚型特异性检测。使用结构化调查问卷收集有关鸟类人口统计特征、来源和健康状况的数据。
主要结果
The overall prevalence of AIV was 14.3% (95% CI: 10.6–18.8), with quail having a higher prevalence (18.2%, 95% CI: 13.0–24.4) than pigeons (7.4%, 95% CI: 3.3–14.1). Among detected subtypes, H9 was most prevalent (11.3%, 95% CI: 7.9–15.4), followed by HA-untyped (2.7%, 95% CI: 1.2–5.1) and H5 (0.3%, 95% CI: 0.001–1.8). Quail had higher odds of AIV positivity than pigeons (aOR = 4.1, 95% CI: 1.8–10.6), and clinically sick birds and those from household farms also had higher odds of AIV positivity.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,鸽子和鹌鹑可能有助于孟加拉国混合家禽生产和销售系统中 AIV 的维持和 传播,这凸显了将这些物种纳入常规 AIV 传播 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,将鸽子和鹌鹑纳入常规 AIV 监测 对于加强早期检测、为控制策略提供信息并降低人畜共患 跨物种溢出 的风险是必要的。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计以及对鸟类所有者和贸易商自我报告数据的依赖可能会引入偏见和混杂因素。样本量和地理范围仅限于孟加拉国吉大港,这可能会影响普适性。

Epidemiological characterization of avian influenza virus circulation in pigeons and quail across different production systems in Chattogram, Bangladesh

Authors: Mohammad Iqbal Habib, Md. Abu Sayeed, Monjurul Islam, Mohammad Ferdous Rahman Sarker, Sydus Shalekin Shakil, Mohammad Mahmudul Hassan, Jade Kenneth Forwood, Abdul Ahad, Ariful Islam
Original abstract (English)
Pigeons and quail contribute to poultry production, nutrition, and livelihoods in Bangladesh; however, their role in avian influenza virus (AIV) circulation remains poorly understood. These poultry species are widely reared and traded alongside chickens and ducks in household farms, commercial systems, and live bird markets, where inadequate biosecurity may create an overlooked ecological niche for AIV circulation, reassortment, and potential zoonotic spillover. Between November 2021 and April 2022, we conducted a cross-sectional study in Chattogram, Bangladesh, to estimate the prevalence and subtype-specific distribution of AIV and to identify factors associated with AIV positivity in pigeons and quail. A total of 300 birds (108 pigeons and 192 quail) were sampled from households, commercial farms, and live bird markets. Oropharyngeal and cloacal swabs were collected from each bird and tested for the AIV matrix gene using real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction, followed by subtype-specific detection of H5, H7, and H9. Data collected from bird owners and traders included bird demographic characteristics, bird source, and health status using a structured questionnaire. The overall prevalence of AIV was 14.3% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 10.6–18.8). Quail had a higher prevalence (18.2%, 95% CI: 13.0–24.4) than pigeons (7.4%, 95% CI: 3.3–14.1). Among detected subtypes, H9 was most prevalent (11.3%, 95% CI: 7.9–15.4), followed by hemagglutinin (HA)-untyped (2.7%, 95% CI: 1.2–5.1) and H5 (0.3%, 95% CI: 0.001–1.8), while H7 was not detected. Quail had higher odds of AIV positivity than pigeons (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 4.1, 95% CI: 1.8–10.6); clinically sick birds (aOR = 2.9, 95% CI: 1.4–6.2) and birds sampled from household farms (aOR = 2.8, 95% CI: 1.2–6.5) also had higher odds of AIV positivity. These findings indicate that pigeons and quails, which are understudied poultry species, may contribute to the maintenance and transmission of AIV within mixed poultry production and marketing systems in Bangladesh. Integrating these species into routine AIV surveillance is therefore necessary to strengthen early detection, inform control strategies, and reduce the risk of zoonotic spillover.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of pigeons and quail in avian influenza virus (AIV) circulation in Bangladesh, addressing a knowledge gap in the epidemiology of AIV in these understudied poultry species.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in Chattogram, Bangladesh, between November 2021 and April 2022, involving 300 birds (108 pigeons and 192 quail) from households, commercial farms, and live bird markets.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal and cloacal swabs were collected from each bird and tested for AIV using real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction, followed by subtype-specific detection of H5, H7, and H9. Data on bird demographic characteristics, source, and health status were collected using a structured questionnaire.
Main results
The overall prevalence of AIV was 14.3% (95% CI: 10.6–18.8), with quail having a higher prevalence (18.2%, 95% CI: 13.0–24.4) than pigeons (7.4%, 95% CI: 3.3–14.1). Among detected subtypes, H9 was most prevalent (11.3%, 95% CI: 7.9–15.4), followed by HA-untyped (2.7%, 95% CI: 1.2–5.1) and H5 (0.3%, 95% CI: 0.001–1.8). Quail had higher odds of AIV positivity than pigeons (aOR = 4.1, 95% CI: 1.8–10.6), and clinically sick birds and those from household farms also had higher odds of AIV positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that pigeons and quails may contribute to the maintenance and transmission of AIV within mixed poultry production and marketing systems in Bangladesh, highlighting the need for integrating these species into routine AIV surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate that integrating pigeons and quails into routine AIV surveillance is necessary to strengthen early detection, inform control strategies, and reduce the risk of zoonotic spillover.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data from bird owners and traders may introduce bias and confounding. The sample size and geographical scope are limited to Chattogram, Bangladesh, which may affect generalisability.

同时检测低致病性 H9N2 禽流感 病毒和沙门氏菌。孟加拉国表现不佳的商业蛋鸡群

Concurrent detection of low-pathogenic H9N2 avian influenza virus and Salmonella spp. in a poorly performing commercial layer flock in Bangladesh
作者： Mohammad Enamul Hoque Kayesh, Md. Kamruzzaman Akimul, Mosharaf Hosen, A. K. M. M. Anower, Md. Rezaul Karim
摘要中文翻译禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 仍然是孟加拉国家禽生产的主要威胁,高致病性 (HPAIV) 和低致病性 (LPAIV) 亚型均在孟加拉国传播。虽然 HPAIV 通常与高 死亡率 相关,但 H9N2 LPAIV 通常会引起轻微的临床疾病,但与商业家禽的产蛋量减少和经济损失有关。本研究描述了 H9N2 和沙门氏菌的同时检测。孟加拉国穆尔维巴扎尔 33 周龄的 Hy-Line Brown 商业蛋鸡群的产蛋性能明显下降。总共对 50 只鸟进行了病毒学和细菌学研究采样。通过针对甲型流感病毒基质 (M) 基因的实时逆转录 PCR (rRT-PCR) 测试口咽拭子,然后进行 H9 和 N2 特异性检测。使用选择性富集和平板接种方法培养泄殖腔拭子,并假定沙门氏菌属。通过针对 invA 基因的 PCR 确认了分离株。在采样的鸟类中,同时存在 H9N2 和沙门氏菌。11/50 中检测到,但没有禽类单独检测出 H9N2 阳性;沙门氏菌属仅在 3/50 的鸟类中检测到。调查鸡群产蛋量明显下降,25周龄一直保持在0%,46周时产蛋率最高仅为71.72%,而品种标准值为88%,而死亡率在46周龄累计达到23.4%,大大超过品种标准。这些发现记录了 H9N2 和沙门氏菌的同时检测。所调查鸡群的产蛋性能较差。鉴于单鸡群设计、缺乏仅 H9N2 对照组和同期健康对照鸡群,以及对其他传染性和农场水平因素的评估不完整,研究结果无法建立因果关系或确定沙门氏菌是否是沙门氏菌。造成了观察到的生产损失。有必要进行涉及多个鸡群和适当比较组的进一步对照和纵向研究,以阐明 H9N2 和细菌混合感染对商业蛋鸡群生产损失的各自影响。

Concurrent detection of low-pathogenic H9N2 avian influenza virus and Salmonella spp. in a poorly performing commercial layer flock in Bangladesh

Authors: Mohammad Enamul Hoque Kayesh, Md. Kamruzzaman Akimul, Mosharaf Hosen, A. K. M. M. Anower, Md. Rezaul Karim
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza virus (AIV) remains a major threat to poultry production in Bangladesh, where both highly pathogenic (HPAIV) and low-pathogenic (LPAIV) subtypes circulate. Although HPAIV is typically associated with high mortality, H9N2 LPAIV often causes mild clinical disease but has been associated with reduced egg production and economic losses in commercial poultry. This study describes the concurrent detection of H9N2 and Salmonella spp. in a 33-week-old Hy-Line Brown commercial layer flock in Moulvibazar, Bangladesh, in which a marked decline in laying performance was observed. A total of 50 birds were sampled for virological and bacteriological investigations. Oropharyngeal swabs were tested by real-time reverse transcription PCR (rRT-PCR) targeting the influenza A virus matrix (M) gene, followed by H9- and N2-specific assays. Cloacal swabs were cultured using selective enrichment and plating methods, and presumptive Salmonella spp. isolates were confirmed by PCR targeting the invA gene. Among the sampled birds, concurrent H9N2 and Salmonella spp. were detected in 11/50, while no birds were positive for H9N2 alone; Salmonella spp. alone was detected in 3/50 birds. The investigated flock exhibited markedly reduced egg production, remaining at 0% through 25 weeks of age and reaching a maximum of only 71.72% at 46 weeks, compared with the breed-standard value of 88%, while cumulative mortality reached 23.4% by 46 weeks, substantially exceeding the breed standard. These findings document the concurrent detection of H9N2 and Salmonella spp. and poor laying performance in the investigated flock. Given the single-flock design, absence of an H9N2-only comparison group and a contemporaneous healthy control flock, and incomplete assessment of other infectious and farm-level factors, the findings do not establish a causal relationship or determine whether Salmonella spp. contributed to the observed production losses. Further controlled and longitudinal studies involving multiple flocks and appropriate comparison groups are warranted to clarify the respective contributions of H9N2 and bacterial coinfections to production losses in commercial layer flocks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza virus (AIV) remains a major threat to poultry production in Bangladesh, where both highly pathogenic (HPAIV) and low-pathogenic (LPAIV) subtypes circulate.
Design and population
This study describes the concurrent detection of H9N2 and Salmonella spp. in a 33-week-old Hy-Line Brown commercial layer flock in Moulvibazar, Bangladesh, in which a marked decline in laying performance was observed.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal swabs were tested by real-time reverse transcription PCR (rRT-PCR) targeting the influenza A virus matrix (M) gene, followed by H9- and N2-specific assays. Cloacal swabs were cultured using selective enrichment and plating methods, and presumptive Salmonella spp. isolates were confirmed by PCR targeting the invA gene.
Main results
Among the sampled birds, concurrent H9N2 and Salmonella spp. were detected in 11/50, while no birds were positive for H9N2 alone; Salmonella spp. alone was detected in 3/50 birds.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings document the concurrent detection of H9N2 and Salmonella spp. and poor laying performance in the investigated flock.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the potential coexistence of H9N2 and Salmonella spp. in commercial layer flocks and their association with reduced egg production, which may have implications for poultry management and disease control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Given the single-flock design, absence of an H9N2-only comparison group and a contemporaneous healthy control flock, and incomplete assessment of other infectious and farm-level factors, the findings do not establish a causal relationship or determine whether Salmonella spp. contributed to the observed production losses.

基于无人机的快速检测在H5N1禽流感暴发期间对白鹤(Grus grus)热点区域的识别中使用深度学习

Rapid UAV-based detection of crane (Grus grus) mortality hotspots during an H5N1 avian influenza outbreak using deep learning
作者： Yotam Nagar, Assaf Chen
摘要中文翻译在传染病暴发期间对野生动物热点区域进行快速检测对于生态风险缓解、人兽共患病风险管理和及时干预至关重要,但实地调查往往耗时、劳动密集且空间受限。本研究评估了结合深度学习的无人机(UAV)图像作为一种操作性工具,用于在以色列胡拉谷地H5N1禽流感暴发期间绘制存活和死亡的普通白鹤(Grus grus)的分布情况。高分辨率RGB图像在水生栖息地和陆地觅食栖息地采集,并进行标注以区分存活和死亡个体。基于YOLOv8的检测框架通过特定栖息地训练、多栖息地联合训练和从大型鸟类数据集进行的迁移学习进行了测试,同时结合选择性数据增强、滑动窗口推理和加权框融合技术。存活的白鹤在不同栖息地中被可靠检测,其在拼接觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.89,在原始觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.98。死亡的白鹤在水体中被准确检测(AP@50≈0.90),但在陆地觅食区的检测仍较为有限(AP@50≈0.64–0.70),这是基于空中标注而没有现场确认的结果。过采样、欠采样和焦点损失提供了有限的改进,表明仅类别不平衡并不能完全解释陆地检测性能较低的原因,而尸体稀缺、视觉多样性有限以及与异质地面背景的模糊性也可能有所贡献。多栖息地联合训练和迁移学习提高了对陆地罕见死亡白鹤类别的泛化能力。这些发现展示了基于无人机的深度学习在野生动物疾病暴发期间进行快速热点识别和评估的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在传染病暴发期间对野生动物热点区域进行快速检测对于生态风险缓解、人兽共患病风险管理和及时干预至关重要,但实地调查往往耗时、劳动密集且空间受限。
设计与对象
本研究评估了结合深度学习的无人机(UAV)图像作为一种操作性工具,用于在以色列胡拉谷地H5N1禽流感暴发期间绘制存活和死亡的普通白鹤(Grus grus)的分布情况。
核心方法
高分辨率RGB图像在水生栖息地和陆地觅食栖息地采集,并进行标注以区分存活和死亡个体。基于YOLOv8的检测框架通过特定栖息地训练、多栖息地联合训练和从大型鸟类数据集进行的迁移学习进行了测试,同时结合选择性数据增强、滑动窗口推理和加权框融合技术。
主要结果
存活的白鹤在不同栖息地中被可靠检测,其在拼接觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.89,在原始觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.98。死亡的白鹤在水体中被准确检测(AP@50≈0.90),但在陆地觅食区的检测仍较为有限(AP@50≈0.64–0.70),这是基于空中标注而没有现场确认的结果。
解释与创新
这些发现展示了基于无人机的深度学习在野生动物疾病暴发期间进行快速热点识别和评估的价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 禽流感 暴发 的快速 监测 方法的开发提供信息,从而能够更有效地监测野生动物种群,并有可能改善人畜共患风险管理。
局限与证据强度
过采样、欠采样和焦点损失提供了有限的改进,表明仅类别不平衡并不能完全解释陆地检测性能较低的原因,而尸体稀缺、视觉多样性有限以及与异质地面背景的模糊性也可能有所贡献。

Rapid UAV-based detection of crane (Grus grus) mortality hotspots during an H5N1 avian influenza outbreak using deep learning

Authors: Yotam Nagar, Assaf Chen
Original abstract (English)
Rapid detection of wildlife mortality during infectious disease outbreaks is essential for ecological risk mitigation, zoonotic-risk management, and timely intervention, yet field surveys are often slow, labor-intensive, and spatially limited. This study evaluates unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery combined with deep learning as an operational surveillance tool for mapping live and deceased Common Cranes ( Grus grus ) during a highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1) outbreak in the Hula Valley, Israel. High-resolution RGB imagery was collected over aquatic roosting and terrestrial feeding habitats and annotated to distinguish living and dead individuals. A YOLOv8-based detection framework was tested using habitat-specific training, combined multi-habitat training, and transfer learning from a large avian dataset, together with selective data augmentation, sliding-window inference, and Weighted Boxes Fusion. Living cranes were detected reliably across habitats, with AP@50 reaching 0.89 in mosaicked feeding-area imagery and 0.98 in raw feeding-area imagery. Dead cranes were detected accurately in water bodies (AP@50 ≈ 0.90), but detection in terrestrial feeding areas remained more limited (AP@50 ≈ 0.64–0.70), based on aerial annotations without in situ confirmation. Oversampling, undersampling, and focal loss provided limited improvement, suggesting that class imbalance alone does not fully explain the lower terrestrial detection performance, and that carcass scarcity, limited visual diversity, and ambiguity against heterogeneous ground backgrounds may also contribute. Joint multi-habitat training and transfer learning improved generalization for the rare dead-crane class on land. These findings demonstrate the value of UAV-based deep learning for rapid mortality assessment and hotspot identification during wildlife disease outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid detection of wildlife mortality during infectious disease outbreaks is essential for ecological risk mitigation, zoonotic-risk management, and timely intervention, yet field surveys are often slow, labor-intensive, and spatially limited.
Design and population
This study evaluates unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery combined with deep learning as an operational surveillance tool for mapping live and deceased Common Cranes (Grus grus) during a highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1) outbreak in the Hula Valley, Israel.

A YOLOv8-based detection framework was tested using habitat-specific training, combined multi-habitat training, and transfer learning from a large avian dataset, together with selective data augmentation, sliding-window inference, and Weighted Boxes Fusion.
Main results
Living cranes were detected reliably across habitats, with AP@50 reaching 0.89 in mosaicked feeding-area imagery and 0.98 in raw feeding-area imagery. Dead cranes were detected accurately in water bodies (AP@50 ≈ 0.90), but detection in terrestrial feeding areas remained more limited (AP@50 ≈ 0.64–0.70), based on aerial annotations without in situ confirmation.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate the value of UAV-based deep learning for rapid mortality assessment and hotspot identification during wildlife disease outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of rapid surveillance methods for avian influenza outbreaks, enabling more efficient monitoring of wildlife populations and potentially improving zoonotic risk management.
Limitations and evidence strength
Oversampling, undersampling, and focal loss provided limited improvement, suggesting that class imbalance alone does not fully explain the lower terrestrial detection performance, and that carcass scarcity, limited visual diversity, and ambiguity against heterogeneous ground backgrounds may also contribute.

低温对亚氯酸水和次氯酸钠溶液灭活材料表面禽流感病毒功效的影响

Impact of low temperature on the efficacy of chlorous acid water and sodium hypochlorite solution for inactivating avian influenza virus on material surfaces
作者： Tomoko Morita, Abeer Mohamed Abdelfattah Elsayed, Rie Hirakawa, Basirat Mojisola Lawal-Ayinde, Tomoyuki Akita, Akima Yamamoto, Akifumi Higashiura, Toshihito Nomura, Isanori Horiuchi, Hisataka Goda
摘要中文翻译亚氯酸水是一种氯基消毒剂,其化学性质与常用的次氯酸钠溶液不同。在本研究中,我们与次氯酸钠溶液相比,评估了亚氯酸水对沉积在材料表面的 禽流感 病毒的灭活效果。首先,在不含氨基酸和蛋白质的条件下检查了两种消毒剂的固有 抗病毒药物 活性。结果表明,99%(IC 99 )、99.9%(IC 99.9 )和99.99%抑制浓度(IC 99.99 )均约为次氯酸钠溶液的一半,表明亚氯酸水具有稍强的灭活活性。接下来,在模拟禽舍环境的条件下评估了对沉积在材料表面(不锈钢、塑料和橡胶)上的含蛋白质病毒的功效。在室温下,两种消毒剂表现出相当的灭活能力;然而,在低温条件下观察到显着差异。具体而言,亚氯酸水的功效得以维持或增强,而次氯酸钠溶液的功效则下降,所需浓度增加约2至3倍。总而言之,这些发现表明,亚氯酸水的 抗病毒药物 活性与次氯酸钠溶液相当或超过,同时即使在低温和存在蛋白质的情况下也能保持其功效。这些特性表明,亚氯酸水可能是现实环境中特别有用的消毒剂,例如在低温条件下运行的禽舍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在与次氯酸钠溶液相比,评估亚氯酸水对材料表面 禽流感 病毒的灭活效果,以满足不同环境条件下对有效消毒剂的需求。
设计与对象
该研究是在受控实验室环境中进行的,旨在使用不锈钢、塑料和橡胶等材料表面,评估两种消毒剂在不同温度条件下和蛋白质存在下对抗 禽流感 病毒的 抗病毒药物 活性。
核心方法
首先在不含氨基酸和蛋白质的条件下测试了两种消毒剂的固有 抗病毒药物 活性。随后,在模拟禽舍条件下评估了它们对材料表面上含有蛋白质的 禽流感 病毒的功效。
主要结果
结果表明,亚氯酸水的99%、99.9%、99.99%抑菌浓度约为次氯酸钠溶液的一半,灭活活性略强。低温条件下,亚氯酸水的药效得以维持或增强,而次氯酸钠溶液的药效则下降,需要浓度增加2~3倍。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,亚氯酸水可能提供与次氯酸钠溶液相当或更好的 抗病毒药物 活性,特别是在低温条件下和存在蛋白质的情况下,这是对现实环境中消毒剂功效的新见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于在禽舍和其他低温和蛋白质污染常见的环境中制定更有效的消毒方案,从而改善疾病预防和控制。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在受控实验室条件下进行的,这可能会限制其对现实世界场景的推广。由于缺乏现场数据和环境因素的潜在变化,证据的强度中等。

Impact of low temperature on the efficacy of chlorous acid water and sodium hypochlorite solution for inactivating avian influenza virus on material surfaces

Authors: Tomoko Morita, Abeer Mohamed Abdelfattah Elsayed, Rie Hirakawa, Basirat Mojisola Lawal-Ayinde, Tomoyuki Akita, Akima Yamamoto, Akifumi Higashiura, Toshihito Nomura, Isanori Horiuchi, Hisataka Goda
Original abstract (English)
Chlorous acid water is a type of chlorine-based disinfectant with chemical properties distinct from those of sodium hypochlorite solution, which is in common use. In this study, we evaluated the inactivation efficacy of chlorous acid water against avian influenza virus deposited on material surfaces, in comparison with sodium hypochlorite solution. First, the intrinsic antiviral activity of both disinfectants was examined under conditions free of amino acids and proteins. The results demonstrated that the 99% (IC 99 ), 99.9% (IC 99.9 ), and 99.99% inhibitory concentrations (IC 99.99 ) were all approximately half those of sodium hypochlorite solution, indicating that chlorous acid water possesses slightly stronger inactivation activity. Next, the efficacy against protein-containing virus deposited on material surfaces (stainless steel, plastic, and rubber) was evaluated under conditions simulating a poultry house environment. At room temperature, both disinfectants exhibited equivalent inactivation capacity; however, a marked difference was observed under low-temperature conditions. Specifically, the efficacy of chlorous acid water was maintained or enhanced, whereas that of sodium hypochlorite solution declined, with the required concentration increasing approximately 2- to 3-fold. Taken together, these findings demonstrate that chlorous acid water exhibits antiviral activity comparable to or exceeding that of sodium hypochlorite solution, while retaining its efficacy even at low temperatures and in the presence of proteins. These properties suggest that chlorous acid water may be a particularly useful disinfectant in real-world settings such as poultry houses operating under low-temperature conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the inactivation efficacy of chlorous acid water against avian influenza virus on material surfaces, in comparison with sodium hypochlorite solution, to address the need for effective disinfectants under varying environmental conditions.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting to assess the antiviral activity of both disinfectants against avian influenza virus under different temperature conditions and in the presence of proteins, using material surfaces such as stainless steel, plastic, and rubber.
Core methods
The intrinsic antiviral activity of both disinfectants was first tested under conditions free of amino acids and proteins. Subsequently, their efficacy was evaluated against protein-containing avian influenza virus on material surfaces under simulated poultry house conditions.
Main results
The results showed that the 99%, 99.9%, and 99.99% inhibitory concentrations of chlorous acid water were approximately half those of sodium hypochlorite solution, indicating slightly stronger inactivation activity. Under low-temperature conditions, the efficacy of chlorous acid water was maintained or enhanced, while that of sodium hypochlorite solution declined, requiring a 2- to 3-fold increase in concentration.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that chlorous acid water may offer comparable or superior antiviral activity to sodium hypochlorite solution, particularly under low-temperature conditions and in the presence of proteins, which is a novel insight for disinfectant efficacy in real-world settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of more effective disinfection protocols in poultry houses and other environments where low temperatures and protein contamination are common, thereby improving disease prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted under controlled laboratory conditions, which may limit its generalizability to real-world scenarios. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of field data and potential variability in environmental factors.

通过数字 PCR 区分废水中的禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR
作者： Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 A(H5Nx) 病毒,特别是进化枝 2.3.4.4b,对全球健康构成重大风险。我们组合并试验了 2 个双工逆转录数字 PCR,以区分废水中的禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒。这种方法能够实现快速、毒株特异性检测,以减少流感病毒环境 监测 的模糊性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种方法将 高致病性禽流感 A(H5Nx) 病毒(特别是进化枝 2.3.4.4b)与废水中的季节性流感病毒区分开来,解决它们造成的全球健康风险。
设计与对象
该研究结合并试验了两种双重逆转录数字 PCR,以区分废水样本中的禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒。
核心方法
该方法能够实现快速、毒株特异性检测,以减少流感病毒环境 监测 的模糊性。
主要结果
该研究成功开发了一种快速、菌株特异性检测废水中禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒的方法。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为提供了一种新颖、快速的方法来区分环境样本中的禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒,这可以减少 监测 中的歧义。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为 监测 和 诊断 提供信息,提供区分废水中禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒的工具,帮助 禽流感 暴发 的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括需要在不同的环境条件下进一步验证以及与其他流感病毒发生交叉反应的可能性,这可能会影响证据的强度。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR

Authors: Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. We combined and piloted 2 duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater. This approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the global health risks posed by highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, by developing a method to differentiate them from seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater.
Design and population
The study combined and piloted two duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater samples.
Core methods
The approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses.
Main results
The study successfully developed a method for rapid, strain-specific detection of avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as providing a novel, rapid method for differentiating avian and seasonal influenza viruses in environmental samples, which can reduce ambiguity in surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnosis by providing a tool to differentiate avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater, aiding in the management of avian influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for further validation in different environmental settings and the potential for cross-reactivity with other influenza viruses, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

预测 禽流感 A (H5N1) 的 进化

Predicting Evolution of Avian Influenza A (H5N1)
作者： Yilin Niu
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒已在禽类群体中引起广泛的 暴发,造成重大经济损失。它们对人类和其他哺乳动物的 跨物种溢出 也引发了对其潜在公共健康影响的担忧。然而,预测禽类 宿主 中 HPAI H5N1 病毒遗传 进化 的模型仍然有限。为了弥补这一差距,我们将仅使用 GISAID 现有的、公开的病毒序列数据和相关元数据来开发和评估基于 Beth-1 的进化预测框架。具体来说,我们将估计连续流感季节中血凝素(HA)蛋白的特定位点适应性景观。我们将利用这些景观来预测遗传变化并选择后续季节的代表性菌株。利用历史序列数据,我们将进行回顾性的逐季预测。将通过比较选定毒株和相应后续季节中观察到的病毒之间的遗传相似性来评估预测性能。本研究旨在将基于适应度的进化预测扩展到禽类 宿主 中的 HPAI H5N1 病毒,并有可能为 疫苗 菌株选择提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决预测禽类 宿主 中 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒 进化 基因的有限模型,该模型造成了重大经济损失并引发了公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用 GISAID 的历史序列数据来开发和评估基于 Beth-1 的进化预测框架,重点关注连续流感季节的血凝素 (HA) 蛋白。
核心方法
历史序列数据和相关元数据用于估计 HA 蛋白的位点特异性适应性景观并预测遗传变化,为随后的季节选择代表性菌株。
主要结果
该研究将进行回顾性季节预测,并通过比较选定菌株和相应后续季节中观察到的病毒之间的遗传相似性来评估预测性能。
解释与创新
该研究旨在将基于适应度的进化预测扩展到禽类 宿主 中的 HPAI H5N1 病毒,并有可能为 疫苗 菌株选择提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 疫苗 菌株选择提供信息,并有可能减少 HPAI H5N1 暴发 对经济和公共卫生的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到历史数据使用的限制,可能无法实时完全捕获 HPAI H5N1 病毒的基因 进化。证据强度中等。

Predicting Evolution of Avian Influenza A (H5N1)

Authors: Yilin Niu
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses have caused widespread outbreaks in avian populations, resulting in significant economic losses. Their spillover into humans and other mammalian hosts has also raised concerns about their potential public health impact. However, models for predicting the genetic evolution of HPAI H5N1 viruses in avian hosts remain limited. To address this gap, we will use only existing, publicly available viral sequence data and associated metadata from GISAID to develop and evaluate an evolutionary forecasting framework based on Beth-1. Specifically, we will estimate site-specific fitness landscapes of the hemagglutinin (HA) protein across successive influenza seasons. We will use these landscapes to predict genetic changes and select representative strains for subsequent seasons. Using historical sequence data, we will conduct retrospective season-to-season predictions. Predictive performance will be assessed by comparing the genetic similarity between selected strains and viruses observed in the corresponding subsequent seasons. This study aims to extend fitness-based evolutionary forecasting to HPAI H5N1 viruses in avian hosts, with the potential to inform vaccine strain selection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited models for predicting the genetic evolution of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses in avian hosts, which have caused significant economic losses and raised public health concerns.
Design and population
The study used historical sequence data from GISAID to develop and evaluate an evolutionary forecasting framework based on Beth-1, focusing on the hemagglutinin (HA) protein across successive influenza seasons.
Core methods
Historical sequence data and associated metadata were used to estimate site-specific fitness landscapes of the HA protein and predict genetic changes, selecting representative strains for subsequent seasons.
Main results
The study will conduct retrospective season-to-season predictions and assess predictive performance by comparing the genetic similarity between selected strains and viruses observed in the corresponding subsequent seasons.
Interpretation and novelty
The study aims to extend fitness-based evolutionary forecasting to HPAI H5N1 viruses in avian hosts, with the potential to inform vaccine strain selection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform vaccine strain selection and potentially reduce the economic and public health impacts of HPAI H5N1 outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of historical data and may not fully capture the genetic evolution of HPAI H5N1 viruses in real-time. The evidence strength is moderate.

2024-2026 年保加利亚 高致病性禽流感 A (H5N1) 的空间分布和遗传多样性

Spatial Distribution and Genetic Diversity of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza A (H5N1) in Bulgaria, 2024–2026
作者： Gabriela V. Goujgoulova, Koycho Koev
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A (H5N1) 仍然是欧洲家禽生产和野生动物健康的重要威胁。本研究旨在描述 2024 年至 2026 年保加利亚 HPAI A (H5N1) 暴发 的空间、时间和遗传模式。使用空间和分子流行病学方法对 44 个确诊的 暴发 进行了分析。使用 Clark-Evans 最近邻分析评估空间聚集,同时使用核密度估计 (KDE) 来识别 暴发 浓度增加的区域。DBSCAN 聚类用于表征空间聚合和地理隔离的 暴发。35 个 暴发 的基因型数据可用,并且使用 Fisher 精确检验评估了基因型与空间组和记录的 宿主 或家禽生产类别的关联。使用系统发育重建进一步分析血凝素(HA)序列。暴发表现出显着的空间聚集,其中最集中发生在保加利亚中南部,次要热点发生在保加利亚东南部。DBSCAN 确定了四个空间集群和八个地理上孤立的 暴发。EA-2023-DA是主要基因型,占35个基因型暴发中的25个(71.4%),并表现出广泛的空间和时间分布。其他基因型,包括 EA-2021-AF、EA-2024-DF、EA-2024-DI DI.2 和 EA-2024-DI DI.2.1,出现频率较低,且空间分布更受限。DBSCAN 空间组之间的基因型组成存在显着差异(Fisher 精确检验,p = 0.0152)。然而,当进行二元基因型比较分析时,EA-2023-DA 在最大空间簇中的富集并不具有统计显着性。基因型与记录的 宿主 或生产类别之间的总体关联显着 (p = 0.0357),但在排除未指定的家禽 (p = 0.2162) 或仅限于鸭和蛋鸡时 (p = 0.7318) 后则不显着。总体而言,研究结果表明保加利亚 HPAI A (H5N1) 暴发 存在明显的空间异质性,并且不同空间组的基因型组成存在显着差异。EA-2023-DA 的广泛检测,以及遗传上不同和地理上更受限制的基因型的出现,表明多种病毒谱系的同时传播。空间、时间和分子 监测 的整合可以支持改进对 暴发 浓度增加的区域的识别,并加强保加利亚的 HPAI 暴发 和控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A (H5N1) 仍然是欧洲家禽生产和野生动物健康的重要威胁。本研究旨在描述 2024 年至 2026 年保加利亚 HPAI A (H5N1) 暴发 的空间、时间和遗传模式。
设计与对象
使用空间和分子流行病学方法对 44 个确诊的 暴发 进行了分析。该研究重点关注 2024 年至 2026 年间保加利亚的 HPAI A (H5N1) 暴发。使用 Clark-Evans 最近邻分析评估空间聚集,同时使用核密度估计 (KDE) 来识别 暴发 浓度增加的区域。
核心方法
35 个 暴发 的基因型数据可用,并且使用 Fisher 精确检验评估了基因型与空间组和记录的 宿主 或家禽生产类别的关联。使用系统发育重建进一步分析血凝素(HA)序列。
主要结果
暴发表现出显着的空间聚集,其中最集中发生在保加利亚中南部,次要热点发生在保加利亚东南部。EA-2023-DA是主要基因型,占35个基因型暴发中的25个(71.4%),并表现出广泛的空间和时间分布。其他基因型,包括 EA-2021-AF、EA-2024-DF、EA-2024-DI DI.2 和 EA-2024-DI DI.2.1,出现频率较低,且空间分布更受限。
解释与创新
EA-2023-DA 的广泛检测,以及遗传上不同和地理上更受限制的基因型的出现,表明多种病毒谱系的同时传播。空间、时间和分子 监测 的整合可以支持改进对 暴发 浓度增加的区域的识别,并加强保加利亚的 HPAI 暴发 和控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过突出 暴发 浓度增加的区域和 HPAI A (H5N1) 的遗传多样性,为保加利亚的针对性 暴发 和控制策略提供信息。该研究提供了对 HPAI A (H5N1) 空间和遗传动态的见解,可能有助于风险评估和预防工作。
局限与证据强度
研究结果表明,保加利亚 HPAI A (H5N1) 暴发 存在明显的空间异质性,并且不同空间组的基因型组成存在显着差异。然而,当进行二元基因型比较分析时,EA-2023-DA 在最大空间簇中的富集并不具有统计显着性。

Spatial Distribution and Genetic Diversity of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza A (H5N1) in Bulgaria, 2024–2026

Authors: Gabriela V. Goujgoulova, Koycho Koev
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A (H5N1) remains an important threat to poultry production and wildlife health in Europe. This study aimed to characterise the spatial, temporal, and genetic patterns of HPAI A (H5N1) outbreaks in Bulgaria during 2024–2026. Forty-four confirmed outbreaks were analysed using spatial and molecular epidemiological approaches. Spatial aggregation was assessed using the Clark–Evans nearest-neighbour analysis, while kernel density estimation (KDE) was used to identify areas of increased outbreak concentration. DBSCAN clustering was applied to characterise spatially aggregated and geographically isolated outbreaks. Genotype data were available for 35 outbreaks, and associations of genotype with spatial group and recorded host or poultry-production category were assessed using Fisher’s exact tests. Hemagglutinin (HA) sequences were further analysed using phylogenetic reconstruction. Outbreaks demonstrated significant spatial aggregation, with the highest concentration occurring in south-central Bulgaria and a secondary hotspot in south-eastern Bulgaria. DBSCAN identified four spatial clusters and eight geographically isolated outbreaks. EA-2023-DA was the predominant genotype, accounting for 25 of the 35 genotyped outbreaks (71.4%), and showed a broad spatial and temporal distribution. Other genotypes, including EA-2021-AF, EA-2024-DF, EA-2024-DI DI.2, and EA-2024-DI DI.2.1, occurred less frequently and showed more restricted spatial distributions. Genotype composition differed significantly among DBSCAN spatial groups (Fisher’s exact test, p = 0.0152). However, the enrichment of EA-2023-DA in the largest spatial cluster was not statistically significant when analysed as a binary genotype comparison. The overall association between genotype and recorded host or production category was significant (p = 0.0357), but not after excluding unspecified poultry (p = 0.2162) or when restricted to ducks and laying hens (p = 0.7318). Overall, the findings demonstrate pronounced spatial heterogeneity in HPAI A (H5N1) outbreaks in Bulgaria, together with significant differences in genotype composition across spatial groups. The widespread detection of EA-2023-DA, alongside the occurrence of genetically distinct and more geographically restricted genotypes, indicates the concurrent circulation of multiple viral lineages. Integration of spatial, temporal, and molecular surveillance can support improved identification of areas with increased outbreak concentration and strengthen HPAI surveillance and control strategies in Bulgaria.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A (H5N1) remains an important threat to poultry production and wildlife health in Europe. This study aimed to characterise the spatial, temporal, and genetic patterns of HPAI A (H5N1) outbreaks in Bulgaria during 2024–2026.

The study provides insights into the spatial and genetic dynamics of HPAI A (H5N1) that may aid in risk assessment and prevention efforts.
