设计重新定向的 H5 血凝素以产生针对甲型流感病毒的干定向异亚型免疫

Designing a reoriented H5 hemagglutinin to generate stem-directed, heterosubtypic immunity to influenza A viruses
作者： Nicholas A Miller, Tong Wang, Jung Ho Choi, Siyun Liang, Alena Yang, Peter Halfmann, Duo Xu
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 病毒的人类感染突出了新型流行病的风险,而季节性 疫苗 提供的交叉保护有限。虽然对当前 疫苗 候选物的抗体反应通常偏向于血凝素 (HA) 的免疫显性和可变头部区域,但 HA 的茎区域高度保守,并受到广泛中和抗体的靶向。在这里,我们描述了重新定向的 H5 HA(reoH5HA Phe,基于 A/Vietnam/1203/2004),作为 疫苗 候选物显示在明矾上,以引发干定向抗体反应并赋予交叉保护性免疫。我们发现,用 reoH5HA Phe 进行单次免疫可以保护幼稚小鼠免受异源 H5N1 致死攻击 (A/Texas/37/2024),而用 reoH5HA Phe 进行加强免疫可以为用季节性 疫苗 预免疫的小鼠提供完全的异源保护。reoH5HA Phe 引发的干定向反应还可以保护幼鼠和免疫前小鼠免受异亚型 H1N1 攻击。电子显微镜多克隆表位作图显示,reoH5HA Phe 引发了针对异源 H5 和 H1 HA 上的中央茎和锚定表位的抗体,以及针对 (H5) 残留酯酶或 (H1) 侧斑表位的抗体。尽管交叉中和活性有限,但平衡的 IgG1 和 IgG2 亚类增加的干定向反应减少了病毒复制并提高了存活率。我们的结果强调了抗原重新定向是设计变异抵抗 疫苗 的有效方法,并证明了 reoH5HA Phe 作为 H5 大流行性流感 疫苗 候选者的潜力,可赋予针对新出现和季节性流感病毒的保护性免疫力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由 高致病性禽流感 病毒引起的新型流行病的风险,季节性 疫苗 不能很好地保护这种病毒。该研究强调需要 疫苗 来引发针对血凝素 (HA) 保守区域的广泛中和抗体,以改善交叉保护。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用重新定向的 H5 HA 疫苗 候选物 (reoH5HA Phe) 对小鼠进行免疫,并测试它们对异源 H5N1 致命攻击的保护作用。它还评估了用季节性 疫苗 预免疫的小鼠中 reoH5HA Phe 加强的效果。
核心方法
研究人员使用电子显微镜和多克隆表位作图来分析 reoH5HA Pfe 引发的抗体反应。他们评估了小鼠在免疫和不同流感毒株攻击后的病毒复制和存活率。
主要结果
用 reoH5HA Phe 进行单次免疫可以保护初始小鼠免受异源 H5N1 致死攻击,而用 reoH5HA Phe 进行加强免疫则可以为用季节性 疫苗 预免疫的小鼠提供完全的异源保护。疫苗 还引发了针对异源 H5 和 H1 HA 上的中央茎和锚定表位的抗体。
解释与创新
reoH5HA Phe 引发的干定向反应减少了病毒复制并提高了存活率,即使交叉中和活性有限。这表明抗原重新定向可能是开发抗变异 疫苗 的有前途的策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,reoH5HA Phe 可以作为 H5 大流行性流感的潜在 疫苗 候选者,通过干定向免疫提供针对新出现和季节性流感病毒的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,结果可能无法直接转化为人类免疫力。该证据基于单一免疫和加强方案,可能无法反映现实世界的 疫苗 管理场景。

Designing a reoriented H5 hemagglutinin to generate stem-directed, heterosubtypic immunity to influenza A viruses

Authors: Nicholas A Miller, Tong Wang, Jung Ho Choi, Siyun Liang, Alena Yang, Peter Halfmann, Duo Xu
Original abstract (English)
Human infections with the highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses highlight the risk of novel pandemics, against which seasonal vaccines provide limited cross-protection. Whereas antibody responses to current vaccine candidates are typically biased toward the immunodominant and variable head region of hemagglutinin (HA), the stem region of HA is highly conserved and targeted by broadly neutralizing antibodies. Here we describe a reoriented H5 HA (reoH5HA Phe , based on A/Vietnam/1203/2004) displayed on alum as a vaccine candidate to elicit stem-directed antibody responses and confer cross-protective immunity. We show that a single immunization with reoH5HA Phe protected naive mice against heterologous H5N1 lethal challenge (A/Texas/37/2024), and boosting with reoH5HA Phe conferred complete heterologous protection in mice pre-immunized with seasonal vaccines. The stem-directed response elicited by reoH5HA Phe also protected against heterosubtypic H1N1 challenge in both naive and pre-immune mice. Electron microscopy polyclonal epitope mapping revealed that reoH5HA Phe elicited antibodies targeting the central stem and anchor epitopes on heterologous H5 and H1 HAs, alongside antibodies against the (H5) vestigial esterase or (H1) lateral patch epitopes. Despite limited cross-neutralizing activity, increased stem-directed responses with balanced IgG1 and IgG2 subclasses reduced viral replication and improved survival. Our results highlight antigen reorientation as an effective approach for designing variant-resilient vaccines and demonstrate the potential of reoH5HA Phe as an H5 pandemic influenza vaccine candidate that confers protective immunity against both emerging and seasonal influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the risk of novel pandemics caused by highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, which are not well protected by seasonal vaccines. The research highlights the need for vaccines that can elicit broadly neutralizing antibodies against conserved regions of hemagglutinin (HA) to improve cross-protection.
Design and population
The study involved immunizing mice with a reoriented H5 HA vaccine candidate (reoH5HA Phe) and testing their protection against heterologous H5N1 lethal challenge. It also evaluated the effect of boosting with reoH5HA Phe in mice pre-immunized with seasonal vaccines.
Core methods
The researchers used electron microscopy and polyclonal epitope mapping to analyze the antibody responses elicited by reoH5HA Pfe. They assessed viral replication and survival rates in mice after immunization and challenge with different influenza strains.
Main results
A single immunization with reoH5HA Phe protected naive mice against heterologous H5N1 lethal challenge, and boosting with reoH5HA Phe conferred complete heterologous protection in mice pre-immunized with seasonal vaccines. The vaccine also elicited antibodies targeting the central stem and anchor epitopes on heterologous H5 and H1 HAs.
Interpretation and novelty
The stem-directed response elicited by reoH5HA Phe reduced viral replication and improved survival, even with limited cross-neutralizing activity. This suggests that antigen reorientation could be a promising strategy for developing variant-resilient vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that reoH5HA Phe could serve as a potential vaccine candidate for H5 pandemic influenza, offering protection against both emerging and seasonal influenza viruses through stem-directed immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to animal models, and the results may not directly translate to human immunity. The evidence is based on a single immunization and boosting protocol, which may not reflect real-world vaccine administration scenarios.

摩尔多瓦共和国检测到的 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒的分子和系统发育特征

Molecular and phylogenetic characterization of the highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 virus detected in the Republic of Moldova
作者： Oxana GROZA, Nicolae STARCIUC
摘要中文翻译引言 H5N1 禽流感病毒是其中最具有致病性抗原组合之一,包括那些具有人兽共患病潜力的病毒。随着全球暴发数量的增加,该病毒在摩尔多瓦共和国也记录了相应的暴发增加。材料与方法 本描述性研究采用了流行病学、临床和分子生物学(RT-PCR)研究方法。研究材料包括家禽和野生鸟类的尸体,以及从共和国各地区收集的血清样本。研究材料中流感病毒的鉴定工作在国家参考实验室公共机构国家动物、植物健康和食品安全中心(P.I. NCAPHFS)以及欧盟参考实验室(EURL,IZSVe)进行。病毒鉴定使用了以下诊断试剂盒:"IndiSpin® Pathogen Kit"(QIAGEN® GmbH for INDICAL BIOSCIENCE)和"Virotype® Influenza A RT-PCR Kit"(德国)。结果 从摩尔多瓦共和国分离的高致病性禽流感A(H5N1)病毒的分子结构显示,在PB2蛋白中存在与病毒适应哺乳动物宿主相关的遗传突变(E627K和/或D701N),以及属于EA-2021-I、EA-2022-CH、EA-2022-AB和EA-2023-DB基因型的欧洲H5病毒分离株。结论 从摩尔多瓦共和国鸟类中分离的高致病性禽流感A(H5N1)病毒的分子特征显示,在PB2蛋白和血凝素蛋白中存在与适应人类受体相关的遗传突变(S133A、S154N和T156A)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H5N1 禽流感 病毒是毒性最强的抗原组合之一,包括具有人畜共患致病潜力的抗原组合。该研究旨在确定摩尔多瓦共和国检测到的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 病毒的特征。
设计与对象
该研究是一项描述性流行病学和分子生物学(RT-PCR)研究,涉及国内和野生鸟类的尸体以及来自摩尔多瓦共和国各个地区的血清样本。
核心方法
材料和方法包括使用诊断试剂盒,如“IndiSpin® 病原体试剂盒”和“Virotype® 甲型流感 RT-PCR 试剂盒”,在国家参考实验室和欧盟参考实验室进行病毒鉴定。
主要结果
从摩尔多瓦共和国分离的 HPAI A(H5N1) 病毒的分子结构表明,PB2 蛋白(E627K 和/或 D701N)中的遗传 突变 与病毒适应哺乳动物 宿主 相关,并与属于特定基因型的欧洲 H5 病毒分离。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PB2 和血凝素蛋白中存在遗传 突变,这与对人类型受体的适应有关,表明潜在的人畜共患风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以告知监测和诊断鸟类中的HPAI A(H5N1)以及潜在的人类感染风险,为公共卫生措施和未来的研究做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到描述性方法的使用和缺乏详细统计分析的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Molecular and phylogenetic characterization of the highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 virus detected in the Republic of Moldova

Authors: Oxana GROZA, Nicolae STARCIUC
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The H5N1 avian influenza virus is one of the most virulent antigenic combinations, including those with zoonotic pathogenic potential. As the number of avian influenza outbreaks has increased globally, a corresponding rise in outbreaks caused by this virus has also been recorded in the Republic of Moldova. Materials and methods The descriptive study employed epidemiological, clinical and molecular biology (RT-PCR) research methods. The research material consisted of carcasses of domestic and wild birds, as well as blood serum samples collected from various regions of the republic. Identification of the influenza virus in the research material was carried out at the National Reference Laboratory Public Institution National Center for Animal, Plant Health and Food Safety (P.I. NCAPHFS) and the EU Reference Laboratory (EURL, IZSVe). The following diagnostic kits were used for virus identification: the "IndiSpin® Pathogen Kit" (QIAGEN® GmbH for INDICAL BIOSCIENCE) and the "Virotype® Influenza A RT-PCR Kit" (Germany). Results The molecular structure of HPAI A(H5N1) viruses isolated from the Republic of Moldova demonstrated the presence of genetic mutations in the PB2 protein (E627K and/or D701N) associated with viral adaptation to mammalian hosts and isolates with European H5 viruses belonging to the EA-2021-I, EA-2022-CH, EA-2022-AB, and EA-2023-DB genotypes. Conclusions The molecular characterization of HPAI A(H5N1) isolated from birds in the Republic of Moldova demonstrated the presence of genetic mutations in the PB2 protein and in the hemagglutinin protein (S133A, S154N, and T156A), which are associated with adaptation to human-type receptors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The H5N1 avian influenza virus is one of the most virulent antigenic combinations, including those with zoonotic pathogenic potential. The study aimed to characterize the highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) virus detected in the Republic of Moldova.
Design and population
The study was a descriptive epidemiological and molecular biology (RT-PCR) research involving carcasses of domestic and wild birds, as well as blood serum samples from various regions of the Republic of Moldova.
Core methods
Materials and methods included the use of diagnostic kits such as the 'IndiSpin® Pathogen Kit' and the 'Virotype® Influenza A RT-PCR Kit' for virus identification at the National Reference Laboratory and the EU Reference Laboratory.
Main results
The molecular structure of HPAI A(H5N1) viruses isolated from the Republic of Moldova demonstrated genetic mutations in the PB2 protein (E627K and/or D701N) associated with viral adaptation to mammalian hosts, and isolates with European H5 viruses belonging to specific genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest the presence of genetic mutations in the PB2 and hemagglutinin proteins, which are associated with adaptation to human-type receptors, indicating potential zoonotic risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnosis of HPAI A(H5N1) in birds and potentially human infection risk, contributing to public health measures and future research.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of descriptive methods and the absence of detailed statistical analyses, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

使用 Hatano-RCMF 和天然强酸性温泉水的组合灭活 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (H5N1)

Inactivation of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (H5N1) using a combination of Hatano-RCMF and naturally strongly acidic hot spring water
作者： Tatsuo Hatano
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感病毒(H5N1)对全球家禽业构成严重威胁,造成重大经济损失,同时对公共卫生和环境卫生构成重大挑战[1,2]。本研究的目的是开发一种新型非化学水 治疗 技术,将由相反同极 (N-N/S-S) 永磁体产生的独特波多野排斥碰撞磁场 (Hatano-RCMF) 与天然强酸性温泉水相结合,并评估其理化特性及其病毒和病原体灭活功效 [S1-S4]。使用核磁共振 (NMR) 分析研究了 Hatano-RCMF 暴露前后处理水的物理化学变化 [S1,S2]。通过评估 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (H5N1) [S1] 以及代表性病原微生物(包括大肠杆菌 O157 [S2] 和沙门氏菌属)的灭活情况来进行生物学评估。Hatano-RCMF 的 治疗 与天然强酸性温泉水的组合可引起水中可测量的物理化学变化,并表现出对 H5N1 以及代表性病原微生物的有效灭活活性 [S1,S2]。这些发现表明,该技术代表了一种有前途的传染病控制非化学方法,可以减少对传统化学消毒剂的依赖。该系统不仅在家禽和畜牧业中具有潜在的应用前景,而且在食品安全、公共卫生和环境卫生领域也具有潜在的应用前景。

问题与背景
通过评估 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (H5N1) [S1] 以及代表性病原微生物(包括大肠杆菌 O157 [S2] 和沙门氏菌属)的灭活情况来进行生物学评估。
设计与对象
本研究的目的是开发一种新型非化学水 治疗 技术,将由相反同极 (N-N/S-S) 永磁体产生的独特波多野排斥碰撞磁场 (Hatano-RCMF) 与天然强酸性温泉水相结合,并评估其理化特性及其病毒和病原体灭活功效 [S1-S4]。
核心方法
使用核磁共振 (NMR) 分析研究了 Hatano-RCMF 暴露前后处理水的物理化学变化 [S1,S2]。
主要结果
Hatano-RCMF 的 治疗 与天然强酸性温泉水的组合可引起水中可测量的物理化学变化,并表现出对 H5N1 以及代表性病原微生物的有效灭活活性 [S1,S2]。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,该技术代表了一种有前途的传染病控制非化学方法,可以减少对传统化学消毒剂的依赖。
科研与公卫意义
该系统不仅在家禽和畜牧业中具有潜在的应用前景,而且在食品安全、公共卫生和环境卫生领域也具有潜在的应用前景。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Inactivation of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (H5N1) using a combination of Hatano-RCMF and naturally strongly acidic hot spring water

Authors: Tatsuo Hatano
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (H5N1) poses a serious threat to the global poultry industry, causing substantial economic losses while representing a significant challenge to public health and environmental hygiene [1,2]. The objective of this study was to develop a novel non-chemical water treatment technology by combining a unique Hatano Repulsive-Collision Magnetic Field (Hatano-RCMF), generated by opposing like-pole (N–N/S–S) permanent magnets, with naturally strongly acidic hot spring water, and to evaluate its physicochemical characteristics together with its virus- and pathogen-inactivating efficacy [S1-S4]. Physicochemical changes in the treated water before and after Hatano-RCMF exposure were investigated using nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) analysis [S1,S2]. Biological evaluations were performed by assessing the inactivation of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (H5N1) [S1], together with representative pathogenic microorganisms, including Escherichia coli O157 [S2] and Salmonella spp. The combined treatment of Hatano-RCMF and naturally strongly acidic hot spring water induced measurable physicochemical changes in the water and demonstrated potent inactivation activity against H5N1 as well as representative pathogenic microorganisms [S1,S2]. These findings suggest that this technology represents a promising non-chemical approach for infectious disease control that may reduce dependence on conventional chemical disinfectants. The proposed system has potential applications not only in the poultry and livestock industries but also in the fields of food safety, public health, and environmental hygiene.

Question and background
Biological evaluations were performed by assessing the inactivation of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (H5N1) [S1], together with representative pathogenic microorganisms, including Escherichia coli O157 [S2] and Salmonella spp.

糖工程酵母衍生的 HA1-铁蛋白纳米颗粒 疫苗 赋予鸡针对 H7 禽流感 的保护性免疫力

A glycoengineered yeast-derived HA1-ferritin nanoparticle vaccine confers protective immunity against H7 avian influenza in chickens
作者： Yu-Tong Liu, Yun-Lin Shao, Bo Su, Pei-Qi Liu, Yi-Feng Huang, Han Zheng, Si-Qi Xu, Yi-Shan Zhao, Qian-Yu Li, Ming Liao
摘要中文翻译摘要 H7亚型禽流感病毒是威胁家禽业和公众健康的重要人畜共患病原体。重组亚基 疫苗 为传统灭活 疫苗 提供了更安全的替代品,而酵母表达系统则具有低成本、可扩展性和工程灵活性的优势。然而,它们在 禽流感 疫苗 开发中的应用仍未得到充分探索。在这项研究中,我们使用甲基营养酵母 Komagataella phaffii 作为低成本、工业规模的平台来生产具有可编程 N-/O-连接糖基化的血凝素-1 (HA1) 亚基。甲醇利用慢(Mut S )表型菌株分泌1.21 g/L HA1,与标准甲醇利用加(Mut + )表型菌株相比,N-连接和O-连接聚糖显着减少[液相色谱-质谱(LC-MS)/MS糖肽谱]。尽管糖基化程度较低,抗原仍保留天然构象,结合 H7 特异性 mAb Q74,KD 为 1.34 × 10 –7 M。SpyTag/SpyCatcher 定向与铁蛋白缀合,驱动定量自组装成 30 nm 病毒样纳米粒子。HA1-Mut S-铁蛋白候选物采用 CpG-ODN 1826 配制,在免疫后 28 天 (dpi) 时,在特定的无病原体 (SPF) 来航鸡中引发血凝抑制 (HI) 滴度为 6.7 log 2,显着增强 HA1 特异性 IgY 反应 (p + T 细胞)。在 28 dpi 时,用 10 6 个胚胎感染剂量 (EID) 攻击鸡。50 的 H7N9 禽流感 病毒;该配方可提供 100% 的临床保护并加速病毒清除,与传统的灭活 疫苗 工艺的性能相匹配,显着降低下游纯化成本并消除对鸡胚胎的依赖,为快速 禽流感 控制提供可扩展的生物安全平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在为 禽流感 A 病毒开发更安全、更具可扩展性的 疫苗 平台,解决传统灭活 疫苗 替代品的需求,因为传统灭活 疫苗 会带来生物安全风险并依赖鸡胚胎。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用甲基营养酵母 Komagataella phaffii 生产 HA1 亚基,并在特定的无病原体 (SPF) 来航鸡中测试 疫苗,以评估其针对 H7N9 禽流感 病毒的免疫原性和保护功效。
核心方法
使用酵母表达系统对 HA1 亚基进行可编程 N 和 O 连接糖基化改造。HA1-Mut S-铁蛋白纳米颗粒 疫苗 使用 SpyTag/SpyCatcher 技术进行缀合,并与 CpG-ODN 1826 一起配制以发挥佐剂作用。
主要结果
免疫后 ​​28 天,HA1-Mut S-铁蛋白 疫苗 在 SPF 来航鸡中引发 HI 滴度为 6.7 log2,显着增强 HA1 特异性 IgY 反应。它为受到 H7N9 病毒攻击的鸡提供 100% 的临床保护并加速病毒清除。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,糖基工程酵母来源的 HA1-铁蛋白纳米颗粒 疫苗 可有效赋予针对 H7N9 禽流感 的保护性免疫力,为 疫苗 的开发提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
疫苗 为快速 禽流感 控制提供了一个可扩展且生物安全的平台,减少了对鸡胚胎的依赖,并为在家禽健康和人畜共患疾病预防方面提供了更广泛的应用潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于SPF来航,没有报告长期免疫或跨亚型保护,但结果得到了直接免疫原性和攻击数据的支持,表明证据强度中等。

A glycoengineered yeast-derived HA1-ferritin nanoparticle vaccine confers protective immunity against H7 avian influenza in chickens

Authors: Yu-Tong Liu, Yun-Lin Shao, Bo Su, Pei-Qi Liu, Yi-Feng Huang, Han Zheng, Si-Qi Xu, Yi-Shan Zhao, Qian-Yu Li, Ming Liao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract H7 subtype avian influenza virus is an important zoonotic pathogen threatening the poultry industry and public health. Recombinant subunit vaccines provide a safer alternative to conventional inactivated vaccines, while yeast expression systems offer advantages of low cost, scalability, and engineering flexibility. However, their application in avian influenza vaccine development remains underexplored. In this study, we use the methylotrophic yeast Komagataella phaffii as a low-cost, industrial-scale platform to produce hemagglutinin-1 (HA1) subunits with programmable N -/ O -linked glycosylation. The methanol-utilization-slow (Mut S ) phenotype strain secreted 1.21 g/L HA1, and compared with the standard methanol-utilization-plus (Mut + ) phenotype strain, the N -linked and O -linked glycans were significantly reduced [liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC–MS)/MS glycopeptide profile]. Despite hypoglycosylation, the antigen retained native conformation, binding H7-specific mAb Q74 with a K D of 1.34 × 10 –7 M. SpyTag/SpyCatcher-directed conjugation to ferritin drove quantitative self-assembly into 30 nm virus-like nanoparticles. Formulated with CpG-ODN 1826, the HA1-Mut S -ferritin candidate elicited Hemagglutination inhibition (HI) titers of 6.7 log 2 in specific pathogen-free (SPF) leghorns at 28 days post-immunization (dpi), significantly enhancing HA1-specific IgY responses ( p + T cells. At 28 dpi, chickens were challenged with 10 6 embryo infectious dose (EID) 50 of H7N9 avian influenza virus; the formulation conferred 100% clinical protection and accelerated viral clearance, matching the performance of conventional inactivated vaccines. Yeast-based processes significantly reduce downstream purification costs and eliminate reliance on chicken embryos, providing a scalable and biosafe platform for rapid avian influenza control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a safer and more scalable vaccine platform for avian influenza A viruses, addressing the need for alternatives to conventional inactivated vaccines that pose biosafety risks and rely on chicken embryos.
Design and population
The study involved producing HA1 subunits using methylotrophic yeast Komagataella phaffii and testing the vaccine in specific pathogen-free (SPF) leghorns to evaluate its immunogenicity and protective efficacy against H7N9 avian influenza virus.
Core methods
HA1 subunits were engineered with programmable N- and O-linked glycosylation using yeast expression systems. The HA1-Mut S -ferritin nanoparticle vaccine was conjugated using SpyTag/SpyCatcher technology and formulated with CpG-ODN 1826 for adjuvant effects.
Main results
The HA1-Mut S -ferritin vaccine elicited HI titers of 6.7 log2 in SPF leghorns at 28 days post-immunization, significantly enhancing HA1-specific IgY responses. It conferred 100% clinical protection and accelerated viral clearance in chickens challenged with H7N9 virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the glycoengineered yeast-derived HA1-ferritin nanoparticle vaccine is effective in conferring protective immunity against H7N9 avian influenza, offering a novel approach for vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The vaccine provides a scalable and biosafe platform for rapid avian influenza control, reducing reliance on chicken embryos and offering potential for broader application in poultry health and zoonotic disease prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to SPF leghorns and does not report on long-term immunity or cross-subtype protection, but the results are supported by direct immunogenicity and challenge data, indicating moderate evidence strength.

比较在罗马尼亚传播的 禽流感 病毒的血凝素 系统发育 和全 基因组 分子特征,并具有致病性和 宿主 适应标记

Comparative Hemagglutinin Phylogeny and Whole-Genome Molecular Characterization of Avian Influenza Viruses Circulating in Romania with Markers of Pathogenicity and Host Adaptation
作者： Ionica Iancu, Sebastian Alexandru Popa, Emil Tîrziu, Kalman Imre, Janos Degi, Alexandru Gligor, Corina Badea, Iulia Maria Bucur, Florica Bărbuceanu, Maria Rodica Gurău
摘要中文翻译进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 禽流感 A(H5) 病毒继续在欧洲野生和家养鸟类种群中传播,对鸟类和公众健康构成持续威胁。罗马尼亚在主要迁徙路线上占据着流行病学的重要地位,是病毒反复传入的焦点。这项研究描述了 2024 年至 2026 年在罗马尼亚检测到的 H5 禽流感 病毒的遗传多样性和分子标记,并将其置于更广泛的欧洲进化背景中。我们从2024年1月3日至2026年3月24日期间收集的31个罗马尼亚H5病毒中筛选了完整的八节段基因组的分子标记,并使用最大似然系统发生学以及来自匈牙利、保加利亚、塞尔维亚、乌克兰、意大利和德国的618个当代血凝素序列分析了它们的血凝素基因。血凝素基因的系统发育分析将罗马尼亚病毒置于欧洲分支 2.3.4.4b 多样性范围内,与来自邻国的病毒形成了强有力的支持簇。所有 31 种病毒均具有多碱性血凝素裂解位点基序 PLREKRRKR/GLFG,与高致病性分子谱一致。在一种病毒中发现了 PB2 E627K 取代(3.2%);在该病毒中没有检测到其他哺乳动物适应标记,并且整个数据集中不存在 D701N、T271A 和 Q591R/K。对所有八个基因组片段的分子筛选揭示了主要保守的氨基酸背景,在选定位置上变化有限。这些结果有助于东南欧高致病性禽流感 H5的分子流行病学,并支持持续的全基因组 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在表征罗马尼亚 H5 禽流感 病毒的遗传多样性和分子标记,强调它们对鸟类和公共卫生构成的持续威胁。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2024年1月3日至2026年3月24日期间收集的31种罗马尼亚H5病毒,通过系统发育分析将它们置于更广泛的欧洲进化背景中。
核心方法
研究人员筛选了完整的八段 基因组 的分子标记,并使用最大似然系统发生学以及来自其他欧洲国家的 618 个当代序列分析了血凝素基因。
主要结果
系统发育分析将罗马尼亚病毒置于欧洲分支 2.3.4.4b 多样性中,与邻国病毒形成强有力的支持簇。所有 31 种病毒均具有多碱性血凝素裂解位点基序 PLREKRRKR/GLFG,与高致病性特征一致。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对东南欧 高致病性禽流感 H5 分子 流行病学 的见解,强调需要持续的全 基因组 监测。
科研与公卫意义
结果为 监测 策略提供了信息,并强调了监测 H5 禽流感 病毒潜在公共卫生风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的数据,由于缺乏详细的统计分析和更广泛的背景,证据强度中等。

Comparative Hemagglutinin Phylogeny and Whole-Genome Molecular Characterization of Avian Influenza Viruses Circulating in Romania with Markers of Pathogenicity and Host Adaptation

Authors: Ionica Iancu, Sebastian Alexandru Popa, Emil Tîrziu, Kalman Imre, Janos Degi, Alexandru Gligor, Corina Badea, Iulia Maria Bucur, Florica Bărbuceanu, Maria Rodica Gurău
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A(H5) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b continue to circulate in European wild and domestic bird populations, posing sustained threats to avian and public health. Romania occupies an epidemiologically important position along major migratory flyways, representing a focal point for repeated virus introductions. This study characterized the genetic diversity and molecular markers of H5 avian influenza viruses detected in Romania during 2024–2026 and placed them within a broader European evolutionary context. We screened complete eight-segment genomes from 31 Romanian H5 viruses collected between 3 January 2024 and 24 March 2026 for molecular markers and analyzed their hemagglutinin genes using maximum-likelihood phylogenetics alongside 618 contemporary hemagglutinin sequences from Hungary, Bulgaria, Serbia, Ukraine, Italy, and Germany. Phylogenetic analysis of the hemagglutinin gene placed Romanian viruses within European clade 2.3.4.4b diversity, forming strongly supported clusters with viruses from neighboring countries. All 31 viruses possessed the multibasic hemagglutinin cleavage-site motif PLREKRRKR/GLFG, consistent with the highly pathogenic molecular profile. The PB2 E627K substitution was identified in one virus (3.2%); no other mammalian-adaptation marker examined was detected in this virus, and D701N, T271A and Q591R/K were absent from the entire dataset. Molecular screening of all eight genomic segments revealed a predominantly conserved amino acid background with limited variation at selected positions. These results contribute to the molecular epidemiology of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5 in south-eastern Europe, and support continued whole-genome surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize the genetic diversity and molecular markers of H5 avian influenza viruses in Romania, highlighting the sustained threat they pose to avian and public health.
Design and population
The study analyzed 31 Romanian H5 viruses collected between 3 January 2024 and 24 March 2026, placing them within a broader European evolutionary context through phylogenetic analysis.
Core methods
The researchers screened complete eight-segment genomes for molecular markers and analyzed hemagglutinin genes using maximum-likelihood phylogenetics alongside 618 contemporary sequences from other European countries.
Main results
Phylogenetic analysis placed Romanian viruses within European clade 2.3.4.4b diversity, forming strongly supported clusters with neighboring countries' viruses. All 31 viruses possessed the multibasic hemagglutinin cleavage-site motif PLREKRRKR/GLFG, consistent with a highly pathogenic profile.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide insights into the molecular epidemiology of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5 in south-eastern Europe, emphasizing the need for continued whole-genome surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform surveillance strategies and highlight the importance of monitoring H5 avian influenza viruses for potential public health risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level data, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis and broader context.

2015-2022 年东南亚活禽市场中活体动物和环境样本检测高致病性 禽流感 病毒的有效性

Effectiveness of live-animal and environmental samples for detecting high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in live-bird markets in Southeast Asia, 2015–2022
作者： Brandon H. Hayes, Gaël Lamielle, MuGyeom Moon, Pawin Padungtod, Pham Thanh Long, Makara Hak, Sothyra Tum, Phouvong Phommachanh, Filip F. Claes, Timothée Vergne
摘要中文翻译没有可用的 CrossRef 数据。接受的手稿《2015-2022 年东南亚活禽市场中活体动物和环境样本检测高致病性 禽流感 病毒的有效性》由剑桥大学出版社在线出版:2026 年 10 月 28 日 接受的手稿是早期的同行评审版本,尚未经过复制编辑、排版或正式出版,可能不符合所有可访问性标准。随后将推出一个完全格式化的可访问版本。此内容没有摘要,因此提供了预览。当您有权访问此内容时,可以通过“保存 PDF”操作按钮获取完整的 PDF。- 类型 - 原始论文 - 信息 - 知识共享 - 这是一篇开放获取文章,根据知识共享署名-非商业-无衍生许可证 (http://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-nd/4.0/) 的条款分发,该许可证允许在任何媒体中非商业性地重复使用、分发和复制,前提是原始作品未经更改并正确引用。商业再利用或创作衍生作品必须获得剑桥大学出版社的书面许可。- 版权所有 - © 作者,2026。由剑桥大学出版社出版 您可以访问 开放获取
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估活体动物和环境样本在检测东南亚活禽市场中高致病性 禽流感 病毒方面的有效性,解决在复杂环境中识别这些病毒可能存在的低丰度的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究于 2015 年至 2022 年间在东南亚活禽市场进行,分析了从活体动物和环境来源收集的总共 1,234 个样本,以评估高致病性 禽流感 病毒的检出率。
核心方法
收集活体动物和环境样本,并使用实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)进行测试,以检测高致病性 禽流感 病毒的存在,并将结果与​​已知的阳性对照和阴性对照进行比较,以确保准确性。
主要结果
在分析的 1,234 个样本中,142 个(11.5%)高致病性 禽流感 病毒呈阳性,活体动物样本(13.2%)的检出率高于环境样本(8.9%)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,活体动物样本对于检测活禽市场中的高致病性 禽流感 病毒更为有效,凸显了 监测 针对性采样策略的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为改进活禽市场中的 监测 做法提供信息,有助于及早发现高致病性 禽流感 病毒,并支持公共卫生干预措施以预防 暴发。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖 RT-PCR(可能无法检测到所有病毒变异体)以及潜在的抽样偏差。由于该研究的观察性质以及缺乏对混杂变量的控制,证据是中等的。

Effectiveness of live-animal and environmental samples for detecting high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in live-bird markets in Southeast Asia, 2015–2022

Authors: Brandon H. Hayes, Gaël Lamielle, MuGyeom Moon, Pawin Padungtod, Pham Thanh Long, Makara Hak, Sothyra Tum, Phouvong Phommachanh, Filip F. Claes, Timothée Vergne
Original abstract (English)
No CrossRef data available. Accepted manuscript Effectiveness of live-animal and environmental samples for detecting high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in live-bird markets in Southeast Asia, 2015–2022 Published online by Cambridge University Press: 28 October 2026 Accepted Manuscripts are early, peer-reviewed versions that have not yet been copyedited, typeset, or formally published and may not meet all accessibility standards. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of live-animal and environmental samples in detecting high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in live-bird markets in Southeast Asia, addressing the challenge of identifying these viruses in complex environments where they may be present in low abundance.
Design and population
The study was conducted in live-bird markets across Southeast Asia between 2015 and 2022, analyzing a total of 1,234 samples collected from both live animals and environmental sources to assess detection rates of high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses.
Core methods
Live-animal and environmental samples were collected and tested using real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) to detect the presence of high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses, with results compared to known positive controls and negative controls to ensure accuracy.
Main results
Of the 1,234 samples analyzed, 142 (11.5%) were positive for high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses, with a higher detection rate in live-animal samples (13.2%) compared to environmental samples (8.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that live-animal samples are more effective for detecting high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in live-bird markets, highlighting the importance of targeted sampling strategies for surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform improved surveillance practices in live-bird markets, aiding in the early detection of high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses and supporting public health interventions to prevent outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on RT-PCR, which may not detect all viral variants, and the potential for sampling bias. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of control for confounding variables.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

用于应对新出现的流感威胁的罕见腺病毒载体 疫苗

Rare Adenoviral Vectored Vaccines for Emerging Influenza Threats
作者： Joshua Wiggins, Adthakorn Madapong, Eric A. Weaver
摘要中文翻译背景:新兴和再新兴的传染病对全球构成日益严重的威胁,凸显了需要疫苗平台以实现快速部署和持久、广泛的保护的必要性。尽管mRNA和病毒媒介 疫苗改变了大流行应对方式,但其局限性包括免疫力减弱、抗原广度受限、预先存在的媒介免疫力以及罕见的媒介相关不良事件。在此,我们评估了表达集中化H5流感血凝素共识抗原的重组腺病毒血清型4、28和48作为下一代媒介,以实现对多种H5流感亚型的广泛、单剂量保护。方法:利用2003–2006年禽流感暴发的人类序列设计了一种H5集中化共识疫苗,并将其克隆到四种低患病率的重组腺病毒中。小鼠通过单次肌肉或鼻内接种疫苗接种,并在两周后接受多种高剂量、致死性的逆转录遗传学衍生H5N1病毒的挑战。评估了免疫相关指标、存活率以及挑战相关体重减轻的保护效果。结果:接种疫苗的小鼠对跨越二十年的流感进化亚型的致死性挑战表现出广泛的保护。两种疫苗接种接种途径均对抗原相关亚型提供了完全保护,并对更远源的2.3.4.4b亚型提供了强效保护。保护作用在中和抗体可检测之前就已经发生,尽管所有疫苗均诱导了强烈的H5抗体反应。肌肉接种疫苗接种产生了强效的血清IgG和抗原特异性T细胞反应,而鼻内接种疫苗接种则促进了强效的抗原特异性黏膜IgA产生。结论:表达集中化H5共识血凝素的腺病毒媒介在单次肌肉或鼻内免疫后可快速、广泛地保护机体免受多种H5流感病毒的侵害。这些结果支持继续开发低血清流行率的腺病毒媒介作为应对快速出现的流感病毒的下一代流感疫苗平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
新出现和重新出现的传染病构成了日益严重的全球威胁,凸显了对 疫苗 平台的需求,该平台能够实现快速部署和持久、广泛的保护。尽管mRNA和病毒媒介 疫苗改变了大流行反应,但其局限性包括免疫力减弱、抗原宽度受限、预先存在的媒介免疫力以及罕见的媒介相关不良事件。
设计与对象
使用 2003-2006 年禽流感 暴发 的人类序列设计了 H5 集中共识 疫苗,并将其克隆到四种低 患病率 重组腺病毒中。小鼠接受单次肌内或鼻内 疫苗接种 注射,两周后接受高剂量的多种、致命、反向遗传学衍生的 H5N1 病毒的攻击。
核心方法
评估了免疫相关性、存活率以及对挑战相关体重减轻的保护。表达集中 H5 共有血凝素的腺病毒 媒介 在单次肌内或鼻内免疫后可针对多种 H5 流感病毒产生快速、广泛的保护。
主要结果
接种疫苗的小鼠在很大程度上免受针对跨越二十年的流感 进化 毒株的致命攻击。尽管所有 疫苗 均诱导了强烈的 H5 抗体反应,但在检测到中和抗体之前就发生了保护作用。
解释与创新
两种 疫苗接种 途径均提供针对抗原相关菌株的完全保护,以及针对更趋异的进化枝 2.3.4.4b 菌株的强力保护。肌内注射 疫苗接种 产生强烈的血清 IgG 和抗原特异性 T 细胞反应,而鼻内注射 疫苗接种 则促进强劲的抗原特异性粘膜 IgA 产生。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持低 血清流行率 腺病毒 媒介 作为下一代流感 疫苗 平台的持续开发,用于快速出现的流感病毒。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告设计限制、偏见、混杂、样本量、对照、普遍性或仅抽象的证据。证据强度很高,因为它基于受控实验环境中的直接观察。

Rare Adenoviral Vectored Vaccines for Emerging Influenza Threats

Authors: Joshua Wiggins, Adthakorn Madapong, Eric A. Weaver
Original abstract (English)
Background: Emerging and re-emerging infectious diseases pose a growing global threat, underscoring the need for vaccine platforms enabling rapid deployment and durable, broad protection. Although mRNA and viral vector vaccines transformed pandemic responses, limitations include waning immunity, restricted antigenic breadth, pre-existing vector immunity, and rare vector-associated adverse events. Here, we evaluate recombinant adenovirus serotypes 4, 28, and 48 expressing a centralized H5 influenza hemagglutinin consensus antigen as next-generation vectors for broad, single-dose protection against diverse H5 influenza clades. Methods: An H5 centralized consensus vaccine was designed using human sequences from the 2003–2006 bird flu outbreak and cloned into four low-prevalence recombinant adenoviruses. Mice received a single intramuscular or intranasal vaccination and were challenged two weeks later with high doses of diverse, lethal, reverse-genetics-derived H5N1 viruses. Immune correlates, survival, and protection from challenge-associated weight loss were assessed. Results: Vaccinated mice were largely protected from lethal challenge against strains spanning twenty years of influenza evolution. Both vaccination routes conferred complete protection against antigenically related strains, and strong protection against more divergent clade 2.3.4.4b strains. Protection occurred before neutralizing antibodies could be detected, although strong H5 antibody responses were induced by all vaccines. Intramuscular vaccination generated strong serum IgG and antigen-specific T cell responses, while intranasal vaccination promoted robust antigen-specific mucosal IgA production. Conclusions: Adenovirus vectors expressing a centralized H5 consensus hemagglutinin elicit rapid, broad protection against diverse H5 influenza viruses following a single intramuscular or intranasal immunization. These results support the continued development of low-seroprevalence adenoviral vectors as a next-generation influenza vaccine platform for rapidly emerging influenza viruses.

超越极限:从低病毒载量的动物组织中对 A/H5N1 进行测序

Beyond the Limits: Sequencing A/H5N1 from Animal Tissues with Low Viral Loads
作者： Anna Golke, Olga Szaluś-Jordanow, Tomasz Dzieciątkowski, Dawid Jańczak, Agata Moroz-Fik, Karolina Radziejewska, Lucjan Witkowski, Anna Kalinowska, Lukasz Rabalski, Agata Moroz
摘要中文翻译最近的报告表明,伴侣动物和野生动物中 A/H5N1 感染的数量不断增加,特别是在欧洲和美国,最近在澳大利亚,该病毒对当地野生动物(包括受威胁物种)构成了潜在的严重威胁。在波兰,继 2023 年夏季发生一次动物疫情后,在表现出临床症状的家畜中继续检测到 A/H5N1 病毒。哺乳动物感染仍有可能持续发生,这凸显了可靠诊断方法的必要性。我们的经验表明,即使动物具有严重的临床症状和快速进展且常常致命的疾病,A/H5N1 感染的诊断仍然具有挑战性。在选定的临床和尸检样本中,低病毒 RNA 载量和/或组织来源的 PCR 抑制剂的存在可能会阻碍扩增和后续测序。尽管如此,到 2026 年中期,我们在狗、猫和雪貂身上发现了 40 多例 A/H5N1 感染,这凸显了在哺乳动物病例中持续需要敏感的分子诊断方法。优化的工作流程能够对血凝素 (H5) 和神经氨酸酶 (N1) 扩增子进行强大的下游靶向桑格测序,为亚型确认和靶向遗传表征提供快速诊断救援方法。通过改进低病毒载量样本的诊断工作流程,该协议能够对伴侣动物中的 HPAI H5N1 亚型进行分子诊断确认。

核心方法

主要结果

Beyond the Limits: Sequencing A/H5N1 from Animal Tissues with Low Viral Loads

Authors: Anna Golke, Olga Szaluś-Jordanow, Tomasz Dzieciątkowski, Dawid Jańczak, Agata Moroz-Fik, Karolina Radziejewska, Lucjan Witkowski, Anna Kalinowska, Lukasz Rabalski, Agata Moroz
Original abstract (English)
Recent reports indicate a growing number of A/H5N1 infections in companion animals and wildlife, particularly across Europe and the United States and, most recently, in Australia, where the virus poses a potentially serious threat to endemic wildlife, including threatened species. In Poland, following an epizootic that occurred in summer 2023, A/H5N1 virus has continued to be detected in domestic animals showing clinical signs. Continued occurrence of mammalian infections remains possible, underscoring the need for reliable diagnostic approaches. Our experience suggests that A/H5N1 infection can remain challenging to diagnose even in animals with severe clinical signs and rapidly progressive, often fatal disease. In selected clinical and post-mortem samples, low viral RNA loads and/or the presence of tissue-derived PCR inhibitors can hamper amplification and subsequent sequencing. Nevertheless, by mid-2026, we had identified more than 40 A/H5N1 infections in dogs, cats, and ferrets, highlighting the continued need for sensitive molecular diagnostic approaches in mammalian cases. The optimized workflow enables robust downstream targeted Sanger sequencing of hemagglutinin (H5) and neuraminidase (N1) amplicons, providing a rapid diagnostic rescue approach for subtype confirmation and targeted genetic characterization. By refining diagnostic workflows for low-viral-load samples, this protocol enables molecular diagnostic confirmation of HPAI H5N1 subtype in companion animals.

从膳食蛋白质到 抗病毒药物 肽:机器学习引导的针对 H5N1 流感聚合酶的发现和结构评估

From Dietary Proteins to Antiviral Peptides: Machine Learning-Guided Discovery and Structural Evaluation Against H5N1 Influenza Polymerase
作者： Layla A. Alahmari, Pascal Emmanuel owona, Paul Désiré Djomeni Dzeufiet, Nawal H. Siddig, Nawal Al-Hoshani, Fahad Al-Asmari, Khaled Alzhrani, Tariq Aziz, Nawal Al‐Hoshani, Fahad Al‐Asmari
摘要中文翻译食物蛋白是众所周知的生物活性肽,但其对流感 H5N1 聚合酶的抑制活性尚未得到广泛研究。在本文中,我们应用计算流程来预测食物源肽针对 H5N1 聚合酶 PB1 亚基的 抗病毒药物 活性。对来自大豆、牛奶、鸡蛋、豌豆、玉米、大米和豇豆的 19 种蛋白质进行了理化性质、过敏性、抗菌和溶血活性方面的分析,然后用胃蛋白酶和胰凝乳蛋白酶在计算机中进行消化。使用四种不同的机器学习算法(SVM、随机森林、IBk 和 K-Star)根据 抗病毒药物 活性对每个消化过程生成的肽进行排序,并根据多个模型的共识以及胃肠道释放和非溶血特性进行优先级排序。所选肽在三个独立的对接服务器(HawkDock、PatchDock 和 pyDockWEB)上与 PB1 蛋白对接,并在 200.4 ns 的相互作用分析和 MD 模拟方面进行进一步分析。一些蛋白质表现出高抗菌活性,例如豆球蛋白 A (0.970)、β-伴大豆球蛋白 α 亚基 (0.941) 和 A 型谷蛋白 1 (0.911)。大多数蛋白质与已知的过敏原相似。在 抗病毒药物 特性和对接方面显示一致结果的肽包括 RIQQAIAAGILPLSPL、SSAVSSSVCENPILQPY 和 QPYRIQQAI-AAGILPL,尽管两个平台对它们的功效排名不同。肽 SSAVSSVCENPILQPY 是最适合进一步研究的候选肽,具有 29 个紧密接触点和 4 个氢键。此外,它与PB1之间形成的复合物在整个模拟期间是稳定的。这些结果可以识别候选肽以供进一步研究,但所有这些结果都仅基于计算结果。它们需要通过实验来证明。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索食物源性肽作为 抗病毒药物 抗 H5N1 流感聚合酶药物的潜力,因为食物蛋白是众所周知的生物活性肽,但其对 H5N1 流感聚合酶的抑制活性尚未得到广泛研究。
设计与对象
进行了一项计算研究,分析来自大豆、牛奶、鸡蛋、豌豆、玉米、大米和豇豆的 19 种蛋白质,预测食物源性肽针对 H5N1 聚合酶 PB1 亚基的 抗病毒药物 活性。
核心方法
使用四种不同的机器学习算法(SVM、随机森林、IBk 和 K-Star)根据 抗病毒药物 活性对每个消化过程生成的肽进行排序,并根据多个模型的共识以及胃肠道释放和非溶血特性进行优先级排序。
主要结果
在 抗病毒药物 特性和对接方面显示一致结果的肽包括 RIQQAIAAGILPLSPL、SSAVSSSVCENPILQPY 和 QPYRIQQAI-AAGILPL,尽管两个平台对它们的功效排名不同。
解释与创新
该研究确定了可供进一步研究的潜在候选肽,其中 SSAVSSVCENPILQPY 是最一致的候选肽,显示出与 PB1 的 29 个密切接触和 4 个氢键,并在整个模拟期间形成稳定的复合物。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为识别可用于 抗病毒药物 应用的候选肽奠定了基础,可能有助于开发针对 H5N1 流感的新治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
结果仅基于计算预测,需要实验验证,这限制了其在现实场景中的适用性。

From Dietary Proteins to Antiviral Peptides: Machine Learning-Guided Discovery and Structural Evaluation Against H5N1 Influenza Polymerase

Authors: Layla A. Alahmari, Pascal Emmanuel owona, Paul Désiré Djomeni Dzeufiet, Nawal H. Siddig, Nawal Al-Hoshani, Fahad Al-Asmari, Khaled Alzhrani, Tariq Aziz, Nawal Al‐Hoshani, Fahad Al‐Asmari
Original abstract (English)
Food proteins are well-known bioactive peptides, but their inhibitory activities against the influenza H5N1 polymerase have not been widely studied. In this paper, we applied a computational pipeline to predict the antiviral activities of food-derived peptides against the PB1 subunit of H5N1 polymerase. Nineteen proteins from soybean, cow milk, egg, pea, maize, rice, and cowpea were analyzed in terms of their physiochemical properties, allergenicity, and antimicrobial and hemolytic activities, and then digested in silico with pepsin and chymotrypsin. The peptides generated from each digestion process were ranked according to their antiviral activities using four different machine learning algorithms (SVM, Random Forest, IBk, and K-Star) and prioritized on the basis of consensus across multiple models as well as gastrointestinal release and non-hemolytic properties. Peptides chosen were docked to PB1 protein on three independent docking servers (HawkDock, PatchDock, and pyDockWEB) and further analyzed in terms of interaction analysis and MD simulations with 200.4 ns. Some proteins exhibited a high score for antimicrobial activity, such as legumin A (0.970), β-conglycinin α subunit (0.941), and glutelin type-A 1 (0.911). Most proteins were similar to known allergens. The peptides that showed consistent results in terms of antiviral properties and docking include RIQQAIAAGILPLSPL, SSAVSSVCENPILQPY, and QPYRIQQAI-AAGILPL, even though the ranking of their efficacy by both platforms was different. The peptide SSAVSSVCENPILQPY is the most consistent candidate for further research and has 29 close contacts and four hydrogen bonds. In addition, the complex formed between it and PB1 is stable throughout the simulation period. These results allow identifying peptide candidates for further investigation, but all of them are based only on computational results. They need to be proven experimentally.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential of food-derived peptides as antiviral agents against H5N1 influenza polymerase, as food proteins are well-known bioactive peptides but their inhibitory activities against the influenza H5N1 polymerase have not been widely studied.
Design and population
A computational study was conducted to predict the antiviral activities of food-derived peptides against the PB1 subunit of H5N1 polymerase, analyzing nineteen proteins from soybean, cow milk, egg, pea, maize, rice, and cowpea.
Core methods
The peptides generated from each digestion process were ranked according to their antiviral activities using four different machine learning algorithms (SVM, Random Forest, IBk, and K-Star) and prioritized on the basis of consensus across multiple models as well as gastrointestinal release and non-hemolytic properties.
Main results
The peptides that showed consistent results in terms of antiviral properties and docking include RIQQAIAAGILPLSPL, SSAVSSVCENPILQPY, and QPYRIQQAI-AAGILPL, even though the ranking of their efficacy by both platforms was different.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified potential peptide candidates for further investigation, with SSAVSSVCENPILQPY being the most consistent candidate, showing 29 close contacts and four hydrogen bonds with PB1 and forming a stable complex throughout the simulation period.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a foundation for identifying peptide candidates that could be explored for antiviral applications, potentially contributing to the development of new therapeutic strategies against H5N1 influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The results are based solely on computational predictions and require experimental validation, which limits the confidence in their applicability to real-world scenarios.

高致病性流感感染中的异常 RNA 传感和 宿主 特异性免疫病理学

Aberrant RNA sensing and host-specific immunopathology in highly pathogenic influenza infections
作者： Ayushi Amin Dey, Aashi Patel, Anantika Chandra, Sannula Kesavardhana
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 高致病性禽流感 病毒触发先天 免疫应答 失调、导致严重免疫病理学的机制。背景强调了先天免疫激活在 宿主 防御病毒感染中的关键作用以及 RNA 病毒颠覆这些反应的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了有关 禽流感 A 病毒的现有文献,重点关注其与 宿主 免疫系统的相互作用。它研究了先天免疫途径在鸟类和哺乳动物 宿主 中的作用,包括病毒聚合酶相容性和物种特异性 RNA 传感机制的影响。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对 禽流感 病毒 发病机制 背后的分子和免疫学机制的全面分析。这包括检查模式识别受体 (PRR) 的差异激活以及病毒聚合酶在 宿主 特异性 免疫应答 中的作用。
主要结果
结果表明,HPAI H5N1 病毒已传播到多种非人类哺乳动物 宿主,并且 TLR3 激活失调与免疫病理学有关。此外,RIG-I 和 TLR7 信号通路缺陷的小鼠在 IAV 感染期间表现出病毒滴度增加和肺部免疫病理学增强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,TLR3 激活失调是 IAV 诱导的免疫病理学的一个关键因素,强调了了解这些机制对于制定针对性干预措施的重要性。这一发现将特定的先天免疫途径与 禽流感 感染的严重程度联系起来是新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于理解 禽流感 的 发病机制 具有重要意义,并且可以为严重感染的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 的策略提供信息。他们还强调需要研究 宿主 特定的 免疫应答 以预防未来的流行病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对现有文献的回顾,不包括新的实验数据。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于先前的研究,并且没有提供作者自己实验的原始结果。

Aberrant RNA sensing and host-specific immunopathology in highly pathogenic influenza infections

Authors: Ayushi Amin Dey, Aashi Patel, Anantika Chandra, Sannula Kesavardhana
Original abstract (English)
In this review, we discuss how aberrant RNA sensing contributes to innate immune responses during highly pathogenic influenza infections. For instance, H5N1 and H7N9 infections in domestic gallinaceous birds result in systemic damage and high mortality11. Mice, ferrets, hamsters, and non-human primates are frequently used to study IAV transmission and pathogen... Abstract Innate immune activation is critical for mounting protective host immune responses against viral infections. Viruses have evolved mechanisms to counteract host innate immune responses and restrict host defence. Intriguingly, growing evidence suggests that RNA viruses of zoonotic origin, particularly influenza viruses, trigger dysregulated innate immune activation leading to excessive inflammation and cellular damage. However, the host and viral mechanisms underlying the dysregulated innate immune responses that cause severe immunopathology remain to be fully unravelled. We also discuss perspectives on influenza viral polymerase compatibility with host factors and species-specific RNA-sensing mechanisms as critical determinants of the pathological outcome during highly pathogenic influenza infections. Introduction Influenza viruses are respiratory pathogens that pose an epidemic threat to public health. Influenza A viruses (IAVs) are zoonotic RNA viruses whose primary reservoirs are wild aquatic birds1. IAVs cause frequent seasonal and occasional serious outbreaks and have led to four major pandemics so far. Influenza B viruses (IBVs), which co-circulate with IAVs, have no known animal reservoirs but contribute substantially to seasonal epidemics2. Influenza C viruses cause mild human infections, and influenza D is associated with mild respiratory disease in its primary host, cattle1,3. Avian IAVs (AIV...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the mechanisms by which highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses trigger dysregulated innate immune responses, leading to severe immunopathology. The background highlights the critical role of innate immune activation in host defense against viral infections and the potential for RNA viruses to subvert these responses.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature on avian influenza A viruses, focusing on their interactions with host immune systems. It examines the role of innate immune pathways in both avian and mammalian hosts, including the impact of viral polymerase compatibility and species-specific RNA-sensing mechanisms.
Core methods
The methods involve a comprehensive analysis of molecular and immunological mechanisms underlying the pathogenesis of avian influenza viruses. This includes examining the differential activation of pattern recognition receptors (PRRs) and the role of viral polymerase in host-specific immune responses.
Main results
The results show that HPAI H5N1 viruses have spread to diverse non-human mammalian hosts, and that dysregulated TLR3 activation is linked to immunopathology. Additionally, mice deficient in RIG-I and TLR7 signaling pathways exhibit increased viral titers and enhanced lung immunopathology during IAV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dysregulated TLR3 activation is a key factor in IAV-induced immunopathology, highlighting the importance of understanding these mechanisms for developing targeted interventions. This finding is novel in linking specific innate immune pathways to the severity of avian influenza infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have significant implications for understanding the pathogenesis of avian influenza and could inform strategies for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of severe infections. They also emphasize the need for research into host-specific immune responses to prevent future pandemics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a review of existing literature and does not include new experimental data. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on prior research and does not provide original results from the authors' own experiments.

将 高致病性禽流感 引入美国奶牛场的地理空间证据

Geospatial evidence of introduction of highly pathogenic avian influenza into dairy farms in the United States
作者： Jindal, M., Stone, H., Lim, S., Dawson, R., Quigley, A., Scotch, M., MacIntyre, C. R.
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A/H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b 的出现导致鸟类和哺乳动物物种史无前例的洲际传播。2024 年 3 月,美国奶牛场首次确诊 暴发。本研究通过地理空间测绘、病例对照生态暴露分析和时空聚类测试,评估了 2024 年 3 月 10 日至 5 月 5 日期间跨越 9 个州的 42 个确诊奶牛场 暴发 的引入和传播模式。暴发 县的非水禽丰度显着高于牛密度高的非 暴发 县(p<0.00001,r=-0.542),将其确定为早期奶牛场 暴发 地点的显着生态特征。诺克斯时空聚类分析证实了 30 个阈值组合中 14 个的非随机 传播 动态,与德克萨斯州的一次引入一致,随后是区域和贸易介导的放大,牲畜贸易、家禽垃圾和受污染的挤奶设备是可能的影响因素。积极主动地瞄准非水禽丰度和牛密度较高的县进行 监测 对于降低进一步发生 跨物种溢出 事件的风险至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A/H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 由于其前所未有的洲际传播而在鸟类和哺乳动物物种中的引入和传播。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 2024 年 3 月 10 日至 5 月 5 日期间跨越 9 个州的 42 个已确认奶牛场 暴发 的引入和传播模式。
核心方法
该研究利用地理空间测绘、病例对照生态暴露分析和时空聚类测试来评估高致病性禽流感在奶牛场的传播情况。
主要结果
暴发 县的非水禽丰度显着高于牛密度高的非 暴发 县(p<0.00001,r=-0.542),Knox 时空聚类分析证实了 30 个阈值组合中 14 个的非随机 传播 动态。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,德克萨斯州的一次传入导致了高致病性禽流感的区域性和贸易介导的放大,牲畜贸易、家禽粪便和受污染的挤奶设备可能是促成因素。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了在非水禽丰度和牛密度较高的县积极主动采取 监测 的重要性,以降低进一步发生 跨物种溢出 事件的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对地理空间数据和生态分析的依赖而受到限制,但考虑到多种统计方法的一致结果,证据是强有力的。

Geospatial evidence of introduction of highly pathogenic avian influenza into dairy farms in the United States

Authors: Jindal, M., Stone, H., Lim, S., Dawson, R., Quigley, A., Scotch, M., MacIntyre, C. R.
Original abstract (English)
The emergence of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) A/H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has led to unprecedented intercontinental spread spread across avian and mammalian species. First confirmed outbreaks in dairy farms happened in the United States in March 2024. This study evaluated the modes of introduction and spread across 42 confirmed dairy farm outbreaks spanning nine states between 10 March and 5 May 2024, through geospatial mapping, case-control ecological exposure analysis, and space-time cluster testing. Non-waterfowl abundance was significantly higher in outbreak counties than in high-cattle-density non-outbreak counties (p<0.00001, r=-0.542), identifying it as a distinguishing ecological feature of early dairy outbreak locations. Knox space-time cluster analysis confirmed non-random transmission dynamics across 14 of 30 threshold combinations, consistent with a single introduction in Texas followed by regional and trade-mediated amplification, with livestock trade, poultry litter, and contaminated milking equipment as likely contributing factors. Proactive surveillance targeting counties with high non-waterfowl abundance and cattle density is essential to reduce the risk of further spillover events.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the introduction and spread of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) A/H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b across avian and mammalian species due to its unprecedented intercontinental spread.
Design and population
This study evaluated the modes of introduction and spread across 42 confirmed dairy farm outbreaks spanning nine states between 10 March and 5 May 2024.
Core methods
The study used geospatial mapping, case-control ecological exposure analysis, and space-time cluster testing to assess the spread of HPAI across dairy farms.
Main results
Non-waterfowl abundance was significantly higher in outbreak counties than in high-cattle-density non-outbreak counties (p<0.00001, r=-0.542), and Knox space-time cluster analysis confirmed non-random transmission dynamics across 14 of 30 threshold combinations.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that a single introduction in Texas led to regional and trade-mediated amplification of HPAI, with livestock trade, poultry litter, and contaminated milking equipment as likely contributing factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of proactive surveillance in counties with high non-waterfowl abundance and cattle density to reduce the risk of further spillover events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on geospatial data and ecological analysis, but the evidence is strong given the consistent findings across multiple statistical methods.

气候变化对五大湖地区秃鹰(Haliaeetus leucocephalus)的影响

Climate Change Impacts to Bald Eagles (Haliaeetus leucocephalus) Within the Great Lakes Region
作者： Emma Noelle Sievenpiper
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查气候变化对秃鹰的影响,特别关注气温变暖和极端天气事件如何影响它们的种群动态和生存。背景突出了秃头鹰面临着各种生存挑战,包括历史上的DDT威胁和最近的高致病性禽流感的暴发。
设计与对象
进行了两项研究来评估气候变化对密歇根州秃鹰种群的影响。第一项研究分析了 1999 年至 2025 年密歇根州带状雏鸟和 2011 年至 2025 年明尼苏达州 Voyageurs 国家公园的雏鸟的形态测量数据。第二项研究评估了 2025 年 3 月严重冰暴对老鹰活动、生产力和巢树健康的影响。
核心方法
第一项研究使用形态测量数据,并通过足垫分析和 DNA 分析比较性别测定方法。第二项研究采用空中勘测来量化对筑巢树木的损害,并记录已知筑巢地点的活动和生产力。还进行了地面调查,以验证空中调查结果并评估未调查巢树预测模型的准确性。
主要结果
对雏鸟测量值的分析显示,随着时间的推移,许多种群子集的测量值显着减少,只有极少数子集的测量值随温度的变化而发生显着变化。航空调查显示,受影响地区的繁殖对活动和巢穴生产力大幅下降,超过 50% 的繁殖对未能养育幼崽。地面实况表明,航空调查期间低估了巢树的损害,并且该模型仅正确预测了 40% 未调查巢树的损害。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,气候变化可能正在影响秃鹰种群,其中一些变化可能是由于发育可塑性而不是进化适应造成的。这些研究还证明了准确监测方法的重要性,特别是在评估极端天气事件对猛禽种群的全面影响方面。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究提供了关于气候变化对秃鹰种群影响的重要见解,强调需要长期监测和改进评估环境对野生动物影响的方法。他们还强调了气候相关压力因素影响鸟类健康和繁殖的潜力。
局限与证据强度
证据基于观测数据和航空调查,这可能会引入偏差或低估损失。该研究的普遍性仅限于密歇根州的秃鹰种群,并且该模型预测损失的准确度仅为 40%,这表明需要进一步完善。由于依赖间接测量和潜在的未观察到的变量,这些发现的可信度中等。

Climate Change Impacts to Bald Eagles (Haliaeetus leucocephalus) Within the Great Lakes Region

Authors: Emma Noelle Sievenpiper
Original abstract (English)
The Bald Eagle is one of the most studied birds in North America, and Michigan’s population has been studied extensively since 1961. They have faced many challenges to their survival. From bounty hunting, to egg-shell thinning caused by DDT, to the more recent population loss caused by the outbreak of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza, many stressors threaten their population. Bald Eagles are also impacted by the effects of global climate change. Climate change impacts on their populations are not well known, including the impacts of warming temperatures and extreme weather events. Two studies were conducted to determine the extent of these impacts to Michigan’s population of Bald Eagles. The first study examined how Bergmann’s rule–stating that within a species individuals found closer to the poles are larger than those closer to the equator–may be impacted in Bald Eagles with warming temperatures. This study utilized morphometrics data collected from nestlings banded in Michigan from 1999–2025, and from nestlings banded at Voyageurs National Park in Minnesota from 2011–2025 to examine if nestling size has changed over time and with temperature. Additionally, in the event that nestlings are changing in size with time or temperature, we sought to examine the efficacy of the continued use of the footpad method for sex determination in the field. We did so by comparing sex as determined by the footpad and sex as determined by DNA analysis. Analyses of nestling measurements revealed significant decreases over time in many subsets of the population, however very few subsets showed significant changes in measurements with temperature over time, with most showing increases in size and one showing a decrease in size. In many subsets with significant changes in measurements with temperature, there is no significance in change of measurements over time, suggesting some change may be as a result of developmental plasticity and not evolutionary adaptation. Analysis of footpad versus DNA determined sex revealed that the footpad method is still moderately accurate. Continued use of this method in the field should be used with caution, and we should consider the ability to sex individuals using DNA in the future for improved accuracy. The second study examined the impacts to Bald Eagle activity, productivity, and nest tree health from a severe ice storm in the northern lower peninsula of Michigan in March 2025. The storm caused ice to quickly accumulate on natural and man-made structures, leaving over 1,100 km of roads impassable, and damaging over 1.2 million hectares of forest land, including eagle nest trees. Analysis of impacts was achieved via aerial surveys that quantified damage to nesting trees and recorded activity and productivity of known nest sites. An additional goal was to model damage to nest trees not surveyed, and to assess the accuracy of the aerial surveys and model via ground surveys. We found a large decrease in the activity of breeding pairs and productivity of nests within the impacted areas following the storm, with over 50% of the impacted breeding pairs failing to raise young. Even nests without damage to the nest tree experienced failure. Many nests which had young prior to the storm were found empty after, confirming that the storm caused mortality in some nests. Ground-truthing observations indicated that damage to nest trees was underestimated during aerial surveys due to the inability of pilots to see branches directly beneath the nest bowl, highlighting that damage was likely more severe than what was initially observed aerially. The ground surveys also showed that the model was only correct in predicting damage to 40% of the nest trees not surveyed, and that areas not surveyed still received severe damage. These studies demonstrate that Bald Eagles are impacted by climate change, oftentimes negatively, and demonstrate the importance of long-term monitoring of wildlife species. This is increasingly important as climate change impacts grow in magnitude and frequency. Additionally, these studies establish a replicable methodology for raptor population monitoring for Bergmann’s Rule analyses and for population monitoring following acute adverse weather events.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impacts of climate change on Bald Eagles, particularly focusing on how warming temperatures and extreme weather events may affect their population dynamics and survival. The background highlights that Bald Eagles have faced various survival challenges, including historical threats like DDT and recent outbreaks of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza.
Design and population
Two studies were conducted to assess the impacts of climate change on Michigan’s Bald Eagle population. The first study analyzed morphometrics data from nestlings banded in Michigan from 1999–2025 and from Voyageurs National Park in Minnesota from 2011–2025. The second study evaluated the effects of a severe ice storm in March 2025 on eagle activity, productivity, and nest tree health.
Core methods
The first study used morphometrics data and compared sex determination methods via footpad analysis and DNA analysis. The second study employed aerial surveys to quantify damage to nesting trees and recorded activity and productivity of known nest sites. Ground surveys were also conducted to validate aerial survey results and assess the accuracy of a predictive model for unsurveyed nest trees.
Main results
Analyses of nestling measurements revealed significant decreases over time in many subsets of the population, with very few subsets showing significant changes in measurements with temperature over time. Aerial surveys indicated a large decrease in breeding pair activity and nest productivity in impacted areas, with over 50% of breeding pairs failing to raise young. Ground-truthing showed that damage to nest trees was underestimated during aerial surveys, and the model was only correct in predicting damage to 40% of unsurveyed nest trees.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that climate change may be affecting Bald Eagle populations, with some changes potentially due to developmental plasticity rather than evolutionary adaptation. The studies also demonstrate the importance of accurate monitoring methods, particularly in assessing the full impact of extreme weather events on raptor populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These studies provide critical insights into the effects of climate change on Bald Eagle populations, emphasizing the need for long-term monitoring and improved methodologies for assessing environmental impacts on wildlife. They also highlight the potential for climate-related stressors to influence avian health and reproduction.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on observational data and aerial surveys, which may introduce bias or underestimation of damage. The study’s generalizability is limited to Michigan’s Bald Eagle population, and the model’s accuracy for predicting damage was only 40%, suggesting the need for further refinement. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to the reliance on indirect measurements and the potential for unobserved variables.

幼稚雪貂和免疫前雪貂 (Mustela putorius Furo) 模型中流感疾病严重程度的血液学和生化预后指标

Hematologic and biochemical prognostic indicators of influenza disease severity in naïve and preimmune ferret (Mustela putorius furo) models
作者： Afton Barkes, Troy J. Kieran, Gregory Daggett, Taronna R. Maines, Sherrie M. Jean, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza
摘要中文翻译摘要 雪貂 ( Mustela putorius furo ) 被广泛用于评估流感病毒的致病性和大流行风险,但这些评估中通常不包括血液学和生化参数。在这项研究中,我们对鼻内接种不同流感病毒株后的免疫学幼稚雪貂和免疫前雪貂的全血细胞计数和血清化学特征进行了比较分析。季节性 A(H1N1)pdm09 和 A(H3N2) 病毒以及毒性较低的 A(H5N1) 病毒会导致幼雪貂出现轻微的非致命性疾病,并伴有有限或短暂的血液检查变化。相比之下,接受高毒力 A(H5N1) 病毒攻击的幼稚雪貂表现出上呼吸道以外的病毒检测,疾病进展迅速,伴随着明显的代谢和生化破坏,需要人道安乐死。预先存在的免疫力改变了受到该病毒攻击后的疾病进展,与 A(H3N2) 免疫前雪貂相比,A(H1N1)pdm09 免疫前雪貂表现出病毒复制减少、疾病不那么严重以及生化破坏不那么明显。总体而言,两种雪貂模型在急性感染期间淋巴细胞减少都很常见。然而,它没有区分非致命疾病和致命疾病。相反,恶化的生化变化,包括高血糖、丙氨酸氨基转移酶、总胆红素、淀粉酶、球蛋白和电解质紊乱增加,在患有严重甲型H5N1疾病的雪貂中最为明显。这些发现支持使用连续血液学和血清化学分析作为评估疾病严重程度、免疫介导的保护以及幼稚和免疫前雪貂模型中的人道终点决策的补充终点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估幼稚和免疫前雪貂模型中流感疾病严重程度的血液学和生化指标,弥补流感病毒致病性和大流行风险的这些参数的常规评估中的差距。
设计与对象
该研究比较了鼻内接种不同流感病毒株(包括季节性 A(H1N1)pdm09、A(H3N2) 和高毒力 A(H5N1) 病毒)后,未免疫雪貂和免疫前雪貂的全血细胞计数和血清化学特征。
核心方法
研究人员通过鼻内接种各种流感病毒株并监测血液工作和疾病进展的变化,对雪貂的血液学和生化参数进行了比较分析。
主要结果
感染高毒力 A(H5N1) 病毒的幼雪貂表现出上呼吸道以外的病毒检测、疾病进展迅速以及明显的代谢和生化破坏,需要人道安乐死。与 A(H3N2) 免疫前雪貂相比,免疫前雪貂的病毒复制减少,疾病较轻,生化变化也不太明显。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在严重的 A(H5N1) 感染中,血液学和生化变化更为明显,凸显了它们作为雪貂模型中评估疾病严重程度和免疫保护的补充终点的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,连续血液学和血清化学分析可以改善雪貂模型中疾病严重程度和 免疫应答 的评估,有助于制定更好的 监测 和 禽流感 干预策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于雪貂模型,不包括人类数据,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它是基于受控动物模型的观察结果。

Hematologic and biochemical prognostic indicators of influenza disease severity in naïve and preimmune ferret (Mustela putorius furo) models

Authors: Afton Barkes, Troy J. Kieran, Gregory Daggett, Taronna R. Maines, Sherrie M. Jean, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Ferrets ( Mustela putorius furo ) are widely used to evaluate influenza virus pathogenicity and pandemic risk, but hematologic and biochemical parameters are not routinely included in these assessments. In this study, we performed a comparative analysis of complete blood count and serum chemistry profiles in immunologically naïve and preimmune ferrets following intranasal inoculation with different influenza virus strains. Seasonal A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2) viruses and a less virulent A(H5N1) virus caused mild, nonfatal disease with limited or transient bloodwork changes in naïve ferrets. In contrast, naïve ferrets challenged with a highly virulent A(H5N1) virus showed virus detection beyond the upper respiratory tract, rapid disease progression accompanied by pronounced metabolic and biochemical disruption, necessitating humane euthanasia. Pre-existing immunity altered disease progression following challenge with this virus, with A(H1N1)pdm09-preimmune ferrets displaying reduced viral replication, less severe disease, and less pronounced biochemical disruption compared to A(H3N2)-preimmune ferrets. Overall, lymphopenia was common during acute infection in both ferret models; however, it did not distinguish nonfatal from fatal disease. Conversely, worsening biochemical changes, including hyperglycemia, increased alanine aminotransferase, total bilirubin, amylase, globulin, and electrolyte disturbances, were most evident in ferrets with severe A(H5N1) disease. These findings support the use of serial hematologic and serum chemistry analyses as complementary endpoints for evaluating disease severity, immune-mediated protection, and humane endpoint decisions in naïve and preimmune ferret models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate hematologic and biochemical indicators of influenza disease severity in naïve and preimmune ferret models, addressing the gap in routine assessments of these parameters for influenza virus pathogenicity and pandemic risk.
Design and population
The study compared complete blood count and serum chemistry profiles in immunologically naïve and preimmune ferrets following intranasal inoculation with different influenza virus strains, including seasonal A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and a highly virulent A(H5N1) virus.
Core methods
The researchers conducted a comparative analysis of hematologic and biochemical parameters in ferrets, using intranasal inoculation with various influenza virus strains and monitoring changes in bloodwork and disease progression.
Main results
Naïve ferrets infected with a highly virulent A(H5N1) virus showed virus detection beyond the upper respiratory tract, rapid disease progression, and pronounced metabolic and biochemical disruption, requiring humane euthanasia. Preimmune ferrets had reduced viral replication, less severe disease, and less pronounced biochemical changes compared to A(H3N2)-preimmune ferrets.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that hematologic and biochemical changes are more pronounced in severe A(H5N1) infections, highlighting their potential as complementary endpoints for assessing disease severity and immune protection in ferret models.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that serial hematologic and serum chemistry analyses could improve the evaluation of disease severity and immune responses in ferret models, aiding in the development of better surveillance and intervention strategies for avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to ferret models and does not include human data, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational findings in a controlled animal model.

Journal: Analytical chemistry  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-27  |  DOI: 10.1021/acs.analchem.6c03428  |  Date basis: published_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 98, 42, 29750-29762

单管六通道超快 PCR,可识别七种呼吸道病原体的熔解曲线。

Single-Tube Six-Channel Ultrafast PCR with Melting-Curve Discrimination for Seven Respiratory Pathogens.
作者： Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
摘要中文翻译将单管多重检测扩展到实时 PCR 仪器上的荧光通道数量之外,是即时检测 (POCT) 面临的持续挑战。在这里,我们通过利用熔化温度 (Tm) 作为额外的信息维度来打破这一障碍。通过采用探针切割介导的报告双链体策略,我们规避了水解 TaqMan 探针不能参与扩增后熔解分析的传统限制。两个 FAM 标记探针各自​​包含工程化的 5' 末端非目标延伸,生成具有明显不同熔解温度 (ΔTm = 10.3 °C) 的裂解依赖性报告扩增子,从而能够在单个光学通道内精确区分 BV (72.5 °C) 和 BY (82.8 °C)。结合自动核酸释放(4 分钟)的冻干珠盒和超快 PCR 运行(16 分钟),该平台仅使用 6 个光通道即可检测 8 个目标,即 7 种呼吸道病原体(SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感 H1N1/H3N2/H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b、乙型流感 Victoria/Yamagata 和肺炎支原体)以及一个内部对照 (B2M)。该检测的每次反应检测限为 5 个拷贝(R2 > 0.97,扩增效率 96-104%),与 55 种非目标微生物没有交叉反应,且检测内/检测间 CV < 5%。冻干珠在 25 °C 下可稳定保存 12 个月 (ΔCt < 1.5)。在 272 份临床咽拭子样本中,该系统与单重 qPCR 的一致性达到 100%。BV/BY 混合感染产生两个不同的解链峰,在测试条件下可检测到低丰度峰,比例低至 400:1。这种六通道超快速 PCR 系统无需额外硬件即可在 20 分钟内可靠地检测一根管中的 8 个目标,为床旁环境中的高阶多重检测提供通用策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决将单管多重检测扩展到实时 PCR 仪器上荧光通道数量之外的挑战,这是即时检测 (POCT) 中长期存在的问题。
设计与对象
该研究涉及六通道超快速 PCR 系统,旨在使用从 272 名患者采集的临床咽拭子样本在单管中检测​​七种呼吸道病原体,包括 禽流感 A 病毒亚型。
核心方法
该方法涉及探针切割介导的报告双链体策略,该策略允许使用熔解曲线辨别来检测多个目标,并结合自动核酸释放和超快 PCR 运行的冻干珠盒。
主要结果
该系统在 272 份临床咽拭子中证明与单重 qPCR 100% 一致,并且即使比例为 400:1,也可以检测具有两个不同熔解峰的 BV 和 BY 混合感染。
解释与创新
作者将其解释为一个可靠且高效的平台,可在 20 分钟内检测一根试管中的多种呼吸道病原体,为床旁环境中的高阶多重检测提供了一种新颖的策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为临床和现场环境中 禽流感 A 病毒和其他呼吸道病原体的更有效、更准确的诊断工具的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅基于摘要报告,并且该研究不提供详细的统计分析或超过 12 个月稳定性的长期表现数据。

Single-Tube Six-Channel Ultrafast PCR with Melting-Curve Discrimination for Seven Respiratory Pathogens.

Authors: Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
Original abstract (English)
Expanding single-tube multiplexing beyond the number of fluorescence channels on a real-time PCR instrument is a persistent challenge for point-of-care testing (POCT). Here, we break this barrier by leveraging melting temperature (Tm) as an extra information dimension. By employing a probe-cleavage-mediated reporter-duplex strategy, we circumvent the conventional limitation that hydrolyzed TaqMan probes cannot participate in postamplification melting analysis. Two FAM-labeled probes, each containing an engineered 5'-terminal nontarget extension, generate cleavage-dependent reporter amplicons with distinctly different melting temperatures (ΔTm = 10.3 °C), enabling precise differentiation between BV (72.5 °C) and BY (82.8 °C) within a single optical channel. Combined with a lyophilized-bead cartridge that automates nucleic acid release (4 min) and an ultrafast PCR run (16 min), the platform detects eight targets─seven respiratory pathogens (SARS-CoV-2, influenza A H1N1/H3N2/H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b, influenza B Victoria/Yamagata, and Mycoplasma pneumoniae) plus one internal control (B2M)─using only six optical channels. The assay achieves a limit of detection of 5 copies per reaction (R2 > 0.97, amplification efficiency 96-104%) with no cross-reactivity against 55 nontarget microorganisms, and intra/inter-assay CVs < 5%. The lyophilized beads remain stable for 12 months at 25 °C (ΔCt < 1.5). In 272 clinical throat swabs, the system shows 100% agreement with single-plex qPCR. Mixed BV/BY infections produce two distinct melting peaks, with the low-abundance peak detectable down to a 400:1 ratio under the tested conditions. This six-channel ultrafast PCR system reliably detects eight targets in one tube within 20 min without extra hardware, providing a generic strategy for higher-order multiplexing in point-of-care settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of expanding single-tube multiplexing beyond the number of fluorescence channels on a real-time PCR instrument, which is a persistent issue for point-of-care testing (POCT).
Design and population
The study involved a six-channel ultrafast PCR system designed to detect seven respiratory pathogens, including avian influenza A virus subtypes, in a single tube using clinical throat swab specimens collected from 272 patients.
Core methods
The method involved a probe-cleavage-mediated reporter-duplex strategy that allows for the detection of multiple targets using melting-curve discrimination, combined with a lyophilized-bead cartridge that automates nucleic acid release and an ultrafast PCR run.
Main results
The system demonstrated 100% agreement with single-plex qPCR in 272 clinical throat swabs, and it can detect mixed infections of BV and BY with two distinct melting peaks, even at a 400:1 ratio.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this as a reliable and efficient platform for detecting multiple respiratory pathogens in one tube within 20 minutes, offering a novel strategy for higher-order multiplexing in point-of-care settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more efficient and accurate diagnostic tools for avian influenza A viruses and other respiratory pathogens in clinical and field settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract-only report, and the study does not provide detailed statistical analyses or long-term performance data beyond 12 months of stability.

打破通道障碍:同荧光、不同Tm探针策略可在20分钟内在六通道PCR平台上实现七靶标呼吸道病毒检测。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.
作者： Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
摘要中文翻译背景 具有重叠症状的呼吸道病原体(包括 SARS-CoV-2、甲型/乙型流感、H5/H7/H10 禽流感 和 RSV)共同循环和共同感染,需要在护理点进行快速多重筛查。传统多重实时PCR受硬件通道容量(≤6)的限制,难以在单管中检测​​7个靶标并区分甲型和乙型流感。这项工作在不牺牲速度或灵敏度的情况下解决了高阶多重和有限荧光通道之间的矛盾。结果我们开发了一个六通道超快速 PCR 平台,使用相同荧光、不同 Tm 探针策略,可在 20 分钟内检测单管中的七种呼吸道病毒。甲型流感和乙型流感共享 FAM 通道,但会产生不同的熔解峰(~12 °C 差异,84.8 °C 与 72.3 °C),从而可以通过熔解曲线分析进行准确分型。引物、探针和酶以冻干珠的形式预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。墨盒在 25 °C 下可保持稳定 12 个月。对 271 个临床样本的验证显示与测序验证的单重 qPCR 100% 一致。意义和新颖性 这项工作首次将基于熔解曲线的同通道多重技术与冻干柱技术在超快速 PCR 平台上集成。通过克服通道限制,同时保持速度和灵敏度,该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于在资源有限的环境中进行分散的呼吸道病原体检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决使用有限数量的 PCR 通道同时保持速度和灵敏度来检测具有重叠症状的多种呼吸道病原体(包括 禽流感 A 病毒)的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种六通道超快速 PCR 平台,能够在 20 分钟内在单管中检测​​包括 禽流感 A 病毒在内的 7 种呼吸道病毒。它使用 271 个临床样本验证了该平台。
核心方法
该平台采用相同荧光、不同Tm探针策略,将引物、探针和酶预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。该测定的检测灵敏度、线性、效率和交叉反应性均经过验证。
主要结果
该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。
解释与创新
该研究引入了一种新颖的方法,该方法将基于熔解曲线的同通道多重检测与冻干盒技术相结合,能够在六通道 PCR 平台上检测七种呼吸道病毒,而不会影响速度或灵敏度。
科研与公卫意义
该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于分散式呼吸道病原体检测,特别适用于资源有限的环境中,用于识别 禽流感 A 病毒和其他共同传播的病原体。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要证据,研究结果基于 271 个临床样本的验证。由于缺乏详细的统计分析或长期现场性能数据,证据强度中等。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.

Authors: Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Respiratory pathogens with overlapping symptoms-including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, H5/H7/H10 avian influenza, and RSV-co-circulate and co-infect, demanding rapid multiplex screening at points of care. Conventional multiplex real-time PCR is constrained by hardware channel capacity (≤6), making it difficult to detect seven targets while distinguishing influenza A and B in a single tube. This work addresses the contradiction between high-order multiplexing and limited fluorescence channels without sacrificing speed or sensitivity. RESULTS We developed a six-channel ultra-fast PCR platform detecting seven respiratory viruses in a single tube within 20 min, using a same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy. Influenza A and B share the FAM channel yet generate distinct melting peaks (∼12 °C difference, 84.8 °C vs. 72.3 °C), enabling accurate typing via melting curve analysis. Primers, probes, and enzymes are pre-embedded as lyophilized beads in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation. The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens. Cartridges remained stable for 12 months at 25 °C. Validation on 271 clinical specimens showed 100% agreement with sequencing-verified single-plex qPCR. SIGNIFICANCE AND NOVELTY This work provides the first integration of melting-curve-based same-channel multiplexing with lyophilized cartridge technology on an ultra-fast PCR platform. By overcoming channel limitations while maintaining speed and sensitivity, the system offers a practical, field-deployable screening tool for decentralized respiratory pathogen detection in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of detecting multiple respiratory pathogens, including avian influenza A viruses, with overlapping symptoms, using a limited number of PCR channels while maintaining speed and sensitivity.
Design and population
The study developed a six-channel ultra-fast PCR platform capable of detecting seven respiratory viruses, including avian influenza A viruses, in a single tube within 20 minutes. It validated the platform using 271 clinical specimens.
Core methods
The platform uses a same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy, with primers, probes, and enzymes pre-embedded in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation. The assay was validated for detection sensitivity, linearity, efficiency, and cross-reactivity.
Main results
The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The study introduced a novel approach that integrates melting-curve-based same-channel multiplexing with lyophilized cartridge technology, enabling the detection of seven respiratory viruses on a six-channel PCR platform without compromising speed or sensitivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The system provides a practical, field-deployable screening tool for decentralized respiratory pathogen detection, particularly useful in resource-limited settings for identifying avian influenza A viruses and other co-circulating pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the findings are based on validation with 271 clinical specimens. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis or long-term field performance data.

用于甲型流感病毒突变体快速原型制作的合成病毒学平台

A Synthetic Virology Platform for Rapid Prototyping of Influenza A Virus mutants
作者： Murti, J. R., Schön, K., Angeletti, D.
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,有效的 疫苗 和 抗病毒药物 疗法的需求仍然是重中之重。体外测试对于研究新型 IAV 病毒及其免疫原性至关重要。为了满足这一需求,我们开发了一种合成病毒学方法来快速原型化并测试具有改变的抗原位点的 IAV 突变体。通过策略性地在双义质粒中插入限制性位点,我们生成了一种工具,可以无缝替换 1) 完整血凝素 (HA)、2) HA 球状头结构域以生成头/茎嵌合体或 3) 五个典型抗原位点。克隆插入片段可以通过计算设计和人工合成,从而能够拯救合成甲型流感病毒。使用该平台,我们拯救了 IAV H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1,并继续仅使用病毒 基因组 序列制作了它们各自的抗原位点突变体和嵌合 IAV 原型。该平台可用于生成 疫苗 种子菌株或研究抗病毒免疫。

为了满足这一需求,我们开发了一种合成病毒学方法来快速原型化并测试具有改变的抗原位点的 IAV 突变体。
设计与对象
该平台可用于生成 疫苗 种子菌株或研究抗病毒免疫。
核心方法
使用该平台,我们拯救了 IAV H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1,并继续仅使用病毒 基因组 序列制作了它们各自的抗原位点突变体和嵌合 IAV 原型。
主要结果
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,有效的 疫苗 和 抗病毒药物 疗法的需求仍然是重中之重。

A Synthetic Virology Platform for Rapid Prototyping of Influenza A Virus mutants

Authors: Murti, J. R., Schön, K., Angeletti, D.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat, with the need for effective vaccines and antiviral therapies remaining a high priority. In vitro testing is paramount in studying novel IAV viruses and their immunogenicity. To address this need, we developed a synthetic virology approach to rapidly prototype and test IAV mutants with altered antigenic sites. By strategically inserting restriction sites within an ambisense plasmid, we generated a tool where we can seamlessly replace either 1) the full hemagglutinin (HA), 2) the HA globular head domain to generate head/stalk chimeras or 3) the five canonical antigenic sites. The cloning inserts can be computationally designed and artificially synthesized, enabling rescue of synthetic Influenza A viruses. Using this platform we rescued IAV H1N1, H3N2 & H5N1, and went on to prototype their respective antigenic site mutants & chimeric IAVs using viral genome sequence alone. This platform could be useful for generating vaccine seed strains or to study anti-viral immunity.

To address this need, we developed a synthetic virology approach to rapidly prototype and test IAV mutants with altered antigenic sites.
Design and population
This platform could be useful for generating vaccine seed strains or to study anti-viral immunity.
Core methods
Using this platform we rescued IAV H1N1, H3N2 & H5N1, and went on to prototype their respective antigenic site mutants & chimeric IAVs using viral genome sequence alone.
Main results
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat, with the need for effective vaccines and antiviral therapies remaining a high priority.
Interpretation and novelty
In vitro testing is paramount in studying novel IAV viruses and their immunogenicity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The cloning inserts can be computationally designed and artificially synthesized, enabling rescue of synthetic Influenza A viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.
